<FILE path="crates/nexus_substrate/src/kernel/muscle.zig">
//! NEXUS SINGULARITY — ZIG MUSCLE v3.2 (2026)
//! Fix: Added missing zx_vmo_create_native and aligned symbols for Rust LLD
const std = @import("std");
const posix = std.posix;
const Thread = std.Thread;

const PAGE_SIZE: usize = 4096;
const MAX_SLOTS: usize = 4096;
const SLOT_MASK: u32 = MAX_SLOTS - 1;
const SENTINEL_MAGIC: u32 = 0xDEADC0DE;
const GENERATION_SHIFT: u5 = 12;
const SHARD_COUNT: usize = 16;
const SHARD_MASK: u32 = SHARD_COUNT - 1;

var shard_mutexes: [SHARD_COUNT]Thread.Mutex = [_]Thread.Mutex{.{}} ** SHARD_COUNT;
var active_vmos: u32 = 0;
var next_handle: u32 = 0xc8;

const VmoMetadata = struct {
    ptr:           [*]align(PAGE_SIZE) u8,
    size:          usize,
    magic:         u32,
    generation:    u32,
    refcount:      u32,
    is_cow_child:  bool,
    parent_handle: u32,
};

const VmoInfo = extern struct {
    handle:          u32,
    size:            usize,
    committed_pages: usize,
    is_cow:          u8,
    parent_handle:   u32,
};

var handle_table: [MAX_SLOTS]?VmoMetadata = [_]?VmoMetadata{null} ** MAX_SLOTS;
var current_load: f32 = 0.0;
var stress_mode: bool = false;

inline fn shard_for(h: u32) usize { return @intCast(h & SHARD_MASK); }
inline fn decode_slot(h: u32) usize { return @intCast(h & SLOT_MASK); }
inline fn decode_generation(h: u32) u32 { return h >> GENERATION_SHIFT; }

fn lookup_valid(h: u32) ?*VmoMetadata {
    const slot = decode_slot(h);
    const gen  = decode_generation(h);
    const meta = &(handle_table[slot] orelse return null);
    if (meta.magic != SENTINEL_MAGIC) return null;
    if (meta.generation != gen)       return null;
    return meta;
}

fn allocate_vmo(size: usize) !struct { ptr: [*]align(PAGE_SIZE) u8, size: usize } {
    const aligned_size = std.mem.alignForward(usize, size, PAGE_SIZE);
    const ptr = try posix.mmap(null, aligned_size, posix.PROT.READ | posix.PROT.WRITE, .{ .TYPE = .PRIVATE, .ANONYMOUS = true }, -1, 0);
    return .{ .ptr = @alignCast(ptr.ptr), .size = aligned_size };
}

export fn zx_vmo_create_native(size: usize) u32 {
    const mapping = allocate_vmo(size) catch return 0;
    const h     = @atomicRmw(u32, &next_handle, .Add, 1, .monotonic);
    const shard = shard_for(h);
    const slot  = decode_slot(h);
    const gen   = decode_generation(h);

    shard_mutexes[shard].lock();
    defer shard_mutexes[shard].unlock();

    handle_table[slot] = VmoMetadata{
        .ptr           = mapping.ptr,
        .size          = mapping.size,
        .magic         = SENTINEL_MAGIC,
        .generation    = gen,
        .refcount      = 1,
        .is_cow_child  = false,
        .parent_handle = 0,
    };
    _ = @atomicRmw(u32, &active_vmos, .Add, 1, .monotonic);
    return h;
}

export fn zx_vmo_clone_native(parent_handle: u32, _: usize) u32 {
    const p_shard = shard_for(parent_handle);
    var parent_size: usize = 0;
    var parent_ptr:  [*]align(PAGE_SIZE) u8 = undefined;

    {
        shard_mutexes[p_shard].lock();
        defer shard_mutexes[p_shard].unlock();
        const meta = lookup_valid(parent_handle) orelse return 0;
        parent_size = meta.size;
        parent_ptr  = meta.ptr;
        _ = @atomicRmw(u32, &meta.refcount, .Add, 1, .monotonic);
    }

    const mapping = allocate_vmo(parent_size) catch return 0;
    @memcpy(mapping.ptr[0..parent_size], parent_ptr[0..parent_size]);

    const h     = @atomicRmw(u32, &next_handle, .Add, 1, .monotonic);
    const shard = shard_for(h);
    const slot  = decode_slot(h);
    const gen   = decode_generation(h);

    shard_mutexes[shard].lock();
    defer shard_mutexes[shard].unlock();

    handle_table[slot] = VmoMetadata{
        .ptr           = mapping.ptr,
        .size          = mapping.size,
        .magic         = SENTINEL_MAGIC,
        .generation    = gen,
        .refcount      = 1,
        .is_cow_child  = true,
        .parent_handle = parent_handle,
    };

    _ = @atomicRmw(u32, &active_vmos, .Add, 1, .monotonic);
    return h;
}

export fn zx_handle_close_native(h: u32) void {
    const shard = shard_for(h);
    shard_mutexes[shard].lock();
    defer shard_mutexes[shard].unlock();

    const meta = lookup_valid(h) orelse return;

    const old_ref = @atomicRmw(u32, &meta.refcount, .Sub, 1, .monotonic);
    if (old_ref == 1) {
        posix.munmap(meta.ptr[0..meta.size]);
        handle_table[decode_slot(h)] = null;
        _ = @atomicRmw(u32, &active_vmos, .Sub, 1, .monotonic);
    }
}

export fn zx_vmo_write_native(h: u32, offset: usize, buffer: [*]const u8, size: usize) i32 {
    const shard = shard_for(h);
    shard_mutexes[shard].lock();
    defer shard_mutexes[shard].unlock();
    const meta = lookup_valid(h) orelse return -1;
    if (offset + size > meta.size) return -1;
    @memcpy(meta.ptr[offset .. offset + size], buffer[0..size]);
    return 0;
}

export fn zx_vmo_read_native(h: u32, offset: usize, buffer: [*]u8, size: usize) i32 {
    const shard = shard_for(h);
    shard_mutexes[shard].lock();
    defer shard_mutexes[shard].unlock();
    const meta = lookup_valid(h) orelse return -1;
    if (offset + size > meta.size) return -1;
    @memcpy(buffer[0..size], meta.ptr[offset .. offset + size]);
    return 0;
}

export fn zx_vmo_get_info(h: u32, out_info: *VmoInfo) i32 {
    const shard = shard_for(h);
    shard_mutexes[shard].lock();
    defer shard_mutexes[shard].unlock();
    const meta = lookup_valid(h) orelse return -1;
    out_info.* = .{
        .handle          = h,
        .size            = meta.size,
        .committed_pages = meta.size / PAGE_SIZE,
        .is_cow          = if (meta.is_cow_child) 1 else 0,
        .parent_handle   = meta.parent_handle,
    };
    return 0;
}

export fn zx_vmo_calculate_entropy(h: u32) f32 {
    const shard = shard_for(h);
    shard_mutexes[shard].lock();
    defer shard_mutexes[shard].unlock();
    const meta = lookup_valid(h) orelse return 0.0;
    var counts = [_]u32{0} ** 256;
    for (meta.ptr[0..meta.size]) |byte| counts[byte] += 1;
    var entropy: f32 = 0.0;
    const size_f: f32 = @floatFromInt(meta.size);
    for (counts) |count| {
        if (count > 0) {
            const p = @as(f32, @floatFromInt(count)) / size_f;
            entropy -= p * std.math.log2(p);
        }
    }
    return entropy / 8.0;
}

export fn zx_vmo_get_all_handles(out_handles: [*]u32, max_count: usize) usize {
    for (&shard_mutexes) |*m| m.lock();
    defer for (&shard_mutexes) |*m| m.unlock();
    var count: usize = 0;
    for (handle_table, 0..) |maybe_meta, i| {
        if (maybe_meta) |meta| {
            if (meta.magic == SENTINEL_MAGIC and count < max_count) {
                const slot: u32 = @intCast(i);
                out_handles[count] = slot | (meta.generation << GENERATION_SHIFT);
                count += 1;
            }
        }
    }
    return count;
}

export fn muscle_get_active_vmos() u32 { return @atomicLoad(u32, &active_vmos, .monotonic); }
export fn muscle_get_next_handle() u32 { return @atomicLoad(u32, &next_handle, .monotonic); }
export fn set_substrate_load(load: f32) void {
    shard_mutexes[0].lock();
    defer shard_mutexes[0].unlock();
    current_load = @max(0.0, @min(1.0, load));
}
export fn set_stress_mode(enabled: bool) void {
    shard_mutexes[0].lock();
    defer shard_mutexes[0].unlock();
    stress_mode = enabled;
}
export fn extract_thermal_pulse_raw() i32 {
    shard_mutexes[0].lock();
    defer shard_mutexes[0].unlock();
    const multiplier: f32 = if (stress_mode) 2.5 else 1.0;
    const temp = (current_load * 40.0 * multiplier) + 30.0;
    return @intFromFloat(temp * 1000.0);
}
</FILE>

#[link(name = "muscle")]
extern "C" {
    pub fn zx_vmo_create_native(size: usize) -> u32;
    pub fn zx_vmo_clone_native(parent_handle: u32, size: usize) -> u32;
    pub fn zx_handle_close_native(handle: u32);
    pub fn zx_vmo_write_native(handle: u32, offset: usize, buffer: *const u8, size: usize) -> i32;
    pub fn zx_vmo_read_native(handle: u32, offset: usize, buffer: *mut u8, size: usize) -> i32;
    pub fn muscle_get_active_vmos() -> u32;
    pub fn muscle_get_next_handle() -> u32;
    pub fn set_substrate_load(load: f32);
    pub fn set_stress_mode(enabled: bool);
    pub fn extract_thermal_pulse_raw() -> i32;
    pub fn zx_vmo_get_info(handle: u32, out_info: *mut VmoInfo) -> i32;
    pub fn zx_vmo_get_all_handles(out_handles: *mut u32, max_count: usize) -> usize;
    pub fn zx_vmo_calculate_entropy(handle: u32) -> f32;
}

pub trait KernelOps: Send + Sync {
    fn vmo_create(&self, size: usize) -> u32;
    fn vmo_clone(&self, parent_handle: u32, size: usize) -> u32;
    fn handle_close(&self, handle: u32);
    fn vmo_get_info(&self, handle: u32, out_info: *mut VmoInfo) -> i32;
    fn set_stress(&self, enabled: bool);
    fn calculate_entropy(&self, handle: u32) -> f32;
}

pub struct NativeKernelOps;
impl KernelOps for NativeKernelOps {
    fn vmo_create(&self, size: usize) -> u32 {
        unsafe { zx_vmo_create_native(size) }
    }

    fn vmo_clone(&self, p: u32, s: usize) -> u32 {
        unsafe { zx_vmo_clone_native(p, s) }
    }

    fn handle_close(&self, h: u32) {
        unsafe { zx_handle_close_native(h); }
    }

    fn vmo_get_info(&self, h: u32, i: *mut VmoInfo) -> i32 {
        unsafe { zx_vmo_get_info(h, i) }
    }

    fn set_stress(&self, enabled: bool) {
        unsafe { set_stress_mode(enabled); }
    }

    fn calculate_entropy(&self, h: u32) -> f32 {
        unsafe { zx_vmo_calculate_entropy(h) }
    }
}

pub enum KernelError {
    VmoCloneFailed { parent: u32, size: usize },
    InvalidArgs,
    RegistryFull,
    AlignmentAnomaly { address: usize },
}

impl std::fmt::Display for KernelError {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
        write!(f, "{:?}", self)
    }
}
impl std::error::Error for KernelError {}

<FILE path="crates/nexus_substrate/src/kernel/safe_vmo.rs">
use crate::kernel::{register_handle, HandleType};
use crate::kernel::ffi;
use crate::kernel::error::KernelError;

pub struct SafeVmo {
    handle: u32,
    pub size: usize,
}

impl SafeVmo {
    pub fn new(size: usize) -> Result<Self, KernelError> {
        let handle = unsafe { ffi::zx_vmo_create_native(size) };
        if handle == 0 {
            return Err(KernelError::InvalidArgs);
        }
        register_handle(handle, HandleType::Vmo(handle));
        Ok(Self { handle, size })
    }

    pub fn write(&self, offset: usize, data: &[u8]) -> Result<(), KernelError> {
        if offset + data.len() > self.size {
            return Err(KernelError::InvalidArgs);
        }
        let status = unsafe {
            ffi::zx_vmo_write_native(self.handle, offset, data.as_ptr(), data.len())
        };
        if status == 0 { Ok(()) } else { Err(KernelError::InvalidArgs) }
    }

    /// ⚡ ZERO-ALLOCATION READ FIX: Prevents heap fragmentation on the 4GB Duet
    pub fn read_into(&self, offset: usize, buffer: &mut [u8]) -> Result<(), KernelError> {
        if offset + buffer.len() > self.size {
            return Err(KernelError::InvalidArgs);
        }
        let status = unsafe {
            ffi::zx_vmo_read_native(self.handle, offset, buffer.as_mut_ptr(), buffer.len())
        };
        if status == 0 { Ok(()) } else { Err(KernelError::InvalidArgs) }
    }

    pub fn handle(&self) -> u32 {
        self.handle
    }
}

impl Drop for SafeVmo {
    fn drop(&mut self) {
        unsafe { ffi::zx_handle_close_native(self.handle) };
    }
}
</FILE>

<FILE path="crates/nexus_substrate/src/vmo_handler.rs">
use axum::{response::IntoResponse, Json};
use serde_json::json;

pub async fn handle_get_vmos() -> impl IntoResponse {
    let mut handles = [0u32; 1024];
    let count = unsafe { crate::kernel::ffi::zx_vmo_get_all_handles(handles.as_mut_ptr(), 1024) };

    let mut vmo_list = Vec::new();
    for i in 0..count {
        let mut info = crate::kernel::ffi::VmoInfo {
            handle: 0,
            size: 0,
            committed_pages: 0,
            is_cow: 0,
            parent_handle: 0,
        };
        let res = unsafe { crate::kernel::ffi::zx_vmo_get_info(handles[i], &mut info) };

        if res == 0 {
            // ⚡ V17: Real-time Entropy Pull
            let entropy = unsafe { crate::kernel::ffi::zx_vmo_calculate_entropy(handles[i]) };

            vmo_list.push(json!({
                "handle": format!("0x{:x}", info.handle),
                "size_bytes": info.size,
                "committed_pages": info.committed_pages,
                "memory_used_kb": (info.committed_pages * 4096) / 1024,
                "is_cow": info.is_cow,
                "entropy": format!("{:.4}", entropy),
                "parent_handle": if info.parent_handle > 0 { format!("0x{:x}", info.parent_handle) } else { "None".to_string() }
            }));
        }
    }

    Json(json!({ "vmos": vmo_list, "active_count": count }))
}

// src/vector_bridge.rs — v1.1 [QUOTA_SHIELDED + MODEL_ALIGNED]
use anyhow::Result;
use reqwest::Client;
use serde_json::json;

pub async fn get_embedding(api_key: &str, text: &str) -> Result<Vec<f32>> {
    // ⚡ SUTURE 1: Shield the API call to respect Free Tier RPM limits
    crate::token_ledger::wait_for_quota("gemini-embedding-2", 500).await;

    let client = Client::new();
    
    // ⚡ SUTURE 2: Align with the models verified by scout_models.sh
    let url = format!(
        "https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models/gemini-embedding-2:embedContent?key={}",
        api_key
    );

    let payload = json!({
        "model": "models/gemini-embedding-2",
        "content": { "parts": [{ "text": text }] }
    });

    let res = client.post(&url).json(&payload).send().await?;
    let body: serde_json::Value = res.json().await?;

    let mut vector = Vec::new();
    if let Some(values) = body.pointer("/embedding/values").and_then(|v| v.as_array()) {
        for v in values {
            if let Some(f) = v.as_f64() {
                vector.push(f as f32);
            }
        }
    } else {
        // Stop swallowing the error so we can debug if it fails again
        return Err(anyhow::anyhow!("API REJECTED EMBEDDING. Raw response: {}", body));
    }

    Ok(vector)
}

pub fn cosine_similarity(a: &[f32], b: &[f32]) -> f32 {
    if a.len() != b.len() || a.is_empty() { return 0.0; }
    let dot: f32 = a.iter().zip(b.iter()).map(|(x, y)| x * y).sum();
    let norm_a: f32 = a.iter().map(|x| x * x).sum::<f32>().sqrt();
    let norm_b: f32 = b.iter().map(|x| x * x).sum::<f32>().sqrt();
    if norm_a == 0.0 || norm_b == 0.0 { return 0.0; }
    dot / (norm_a * norm_b)
}

<FILE path="crates/nexus_substrate/src/token_ledger.rs">
use once_cell::sync::Lazy;
use std::collections::HashMap;
use std::sync::Mutex;
use std::time::{Duration, Instant, SystemTime};

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct ModelQuota {
    pub rpm_limit: u32,
    pub tpm_limit: u32,
}

impl ModelQuota {
    fn for_model(model: &str) -> Self {
        match model {
            // Dashboard: 5 RPM. Shielding at 4.
            m if m.contains("flash") => Self { rpm_limit: 4, tpm_limit: 240_000 },
            // Dashboard: 15 RPM. Shielding at 14.
            m if m.contains("31b")   => Self { rpm_limit: 14, tpm_limit: 100_000 },
            // Dashboard: 15 RPM. Shielding at 14.
            m if m.contains("26b")   => Self { rpm_limit: 14, tpm_limit: 100_000 },
            // Dashboard: 30 RPM. Shielding at 28.
            m if m.contains("27b")   => Self { rpm_limit: 28, tpm_limit: 14_000 },
            // Universal fallback
            _                        => Self { rpm_limit: 10, tpm_limit: 50_000 },
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Debug)]
struct WindowEntry {
    timestamp: Instant,
    tokens:    u32,
}

#[derive(Debug, Default)]
struct ModelWindow {
    entries: Vec<WindowEntry>,
}

impl ModelWindow {
    fn purge_stale(&mut self) {
        let cutoff = Instant::now() - Duration::from_secs(60);
        self.entries.retain(|e| e.timestamp > cutoff);
    }

    fn token_sum(&self) -> u32 {
        self.entries.iter().map(|e| e.tokens).sum()
    }

    fn ms_until_slot_free(&self) -> u64 {
        if self.entries.is_empty() { return 0; }
        let oldest = self.entries.iter().map(|e| e.timestamp).min().unwrap();
        (Duration::from_secs(60).saturating_sub(oldest.elapsed())).as_millis() as u64 + 100
    }
}

pub struct TokenLedger {
    windows: Mutex<HashMap<String, ModelWindow>>,
}

impl TokenLedger {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self { windows: Mutex::new(HashMap::new()) }
    }

    pub fn check_and_reserve(&self, model: &str, estimated_tokens: u32) -> Result<(), u64> {
        let quota = ModelQuota::for_model(model);
        let mut map = self.windows.lock().unwrap();
        let window = map.entry(model.to_string()).or_default();
        window.purge_stale();

        if window.entries.len() as u32 >= quota.rpm_limit { return Err(window.ms_until_slot_free()); }
        if window.token_sum() + estimated_tokens > quota.tpm_limit { return Err(window.ms_until_slot_free()); }

        window.entries.push(WindowEntry { timestamp: Instant::now(), tokens: estimated_tokens });
        Ok(())
    }
}

pub static LEDGER: Lazy<TokenLedger> = Lazy::new(TokenLedger::new);

pub async fn wait_for_quota(model: &str, estimated_tokens: u32) {
    let mut attempts = 0;
    loop {
        match LEDGER.check_and_reserve(model, estimated_tokens) {
            Ok(()) => return,
            Err(wait_ms) => {
                let jitter = (generate_seed() % 500) as u64;
                println!(
                    "\x1b[33m[LEDGER] {} ceiling hit. Waiting {}ms (attempt {})...\x1b[0m",
                    model, wait_ms + jitter, attempts + 1
                );
                tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(wait_ms + jitter)).await;
                attempts += 1;
                if attempts > 10 { break; }
            }
        }
    }
}

fn http_rpm_limit() -> u32 {
    std::env::var("NEXUS_HTTP_RPM").ok().and_then(|v| v.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(60)
}

static HTTP_WINDOWS: Lazy<Mutex<HashMap<String, Vec<Instant>>>> = Lazy::new(|| Mutex::new(HashMap::new()));

pub fn http_check(ip: &str) -> Result<(), u64> {
    let limit = http_rpm_limit();
    let cutoff = Instant::now() - Duration::from_secs(60);

    let mut map = HTTP_WINDOWS.lock().unwrap();
    let window = map.entry(ip.to_string()).or_default();
    window.retain(|&ts| ts > cutoff);

    if window.len() as u32 >= limit {
        let oldest = *window.first().unwrap();
        let wait = (Duration::from_secs(60).saturating_sub(oldest.elapsed())).as_millis() as u64;
        return Err(wait + 100);
    }

    window.push(Instant::now());
    Ok(())
}

/// generate_seed: OS-entropy random u64 with timestamp mixing for per-call uniqueness.
/// RandomState seeds from getrandom() syscall; writing the current nanos ensures
/// two calls within the same nanosecond still return different values.
fn generate_seed() -> u64 {
    use std::hash::{BuildHasher, Hasher};
    let t = SystemTime::now()
        .duration_since(SystemTime::UNIX_EPOCH)
        .unwrap_or_default()
        .as_nanos() as u64;
    let mut h = std::collections::hash_map::RandomState::new().build_hasher();
    h.write_u64(t);
    h.finish()
}
</FILE>

<FILE path="crates/nexus_substrate/src/memory.rs">
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use sqlx::{sqlite::SqlitePoolOptions, Row, SqlitePool};
use std::path::Path;

#[derive(Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct AgentMemory {
    pub id: i64,
    pub origin: String,
    pub directive: String,
    pub result: String,
    pub timestamp: String,
}

pub struct MemoryPalace {
    pub pool: SqlitePool,
}

impl MemoryPalace {
    pub async fn new(db_path: &str) -> Result<Self, sqlx::Error> {
        if !Path::new(db_path).exists() { std::fs::File::create(db_path).ok(); }
        let pool = SqlitePoolOptions::new()
            .max_connections(5)
            .connect(&format!("sqlite:{}", db_path))
            .await?;

        sqlx::query("PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL;").execute(&pool).await?;
        sqlx::query("PRAGMA cache_size=-16000;").execute(&pool).await?;
        sqlx::query("PRAGMA synchronous=NORMAL;").execute(&pool).await?;

        let palace = Self { pool };
        palace.init_full_schema().await?;
        Ok(palace)
    }

    pub async fn init_full_schema(&self) -> Result<(), sqlx::Error> {
        sqlx::query(
            "CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS neural_context (
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
            origin TEXT NOT NULL,
            directive TEXT NOT NULL,
            result TEXT NOT NULL,
            embedding TEXT,
            timestamp DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
        );",
        ).execute(&self.pool).await?;

        sqlx::query(
            "CREATE VIRTUAL TABLE IF NOT EXISTS memory_search USING fts5(
            directive, result, content='neural_context', content_rowid='id'
        );",
        ).execute(&self.pool).await?;

        sqlx::query(
            "CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS system_insights (
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
            summary TEXT NOT NULL,
            timestamp DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
        );",
        ).execute(&self.pool).await?;

        Ok(())
    }

    /// SUTURE: Atomic Transaction over FTS and Base Table
    pub async fn store_memory(
        &self,
        origin: &str,
        directive: &str,
        result: &str,
        embedding: Option<Vec<f32>>,
    ) -> Result<(), sqlx::Error> {
        let emb_str = embedding.map(|v| serde_json::to_string(&v).unwrap_or_default());
        
        let mut tx = self.pool.begin().await?;

        let res = sqlx::query("INSERT INTO neural_context (origin, directive, result, embedding) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)")
            .bind(origin)
            .bind(directive)
            .bind(result)
            .bind(emb_str)
            .execute(&mut *tx)
            .await?;

        let row_id = res.last_insert_rowid();
        sqlx::query("INSERT INTO memory_search (rowid, directive, result) VALUES (?, ?, ?)")
            .bind(row_id)
            .bind(directive)
            .bind(result)
            .execute(&mut *tx)
            .await?;
            
        tx.commit().await?;
        Ok(())
    }

    pub async fn store_insight(&self, summary: &str) -> Result<(), sqlx::Error> {
        sqlx::query("INSERT INTO system_insights (summary) VALUES (?)").bind(summary).execute(&self.pool).await?;
        Ok(())
    }

    pub async fn fetch_all(&self) -> Result<Vec<AgentMemory>, sqlx::Error> {
        let rows = sqlx::query("SELECT id, origin, directive, result, timestamp FROM neural_context ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 50")
            .fetch_all(&self.pool).await?;
        Ok(rows.into_iter().map(|row| AgentMemory {
            id: row.get("id"),
            origin: row.get("origin"),
            directive: row.get("directive"),
            result: row.get("result"),
            timestamp: row.get("timestamp"),
        }).collect())
    }

    pub async fn search_memories(&self, query: &str) -> Result<Vec<AgentMemory>, sqlx::Error> {
        let rows = sqlx::query(
            "SELECT n.id, n.origin, n.directive, n.result, n.timestamp 
             FROM neural_context n JOIN memory_search m ON n.id = m.rowid 
             WHERE memory_search MATCH ? ORDER BY rank LIMIT 10",
        ).bind(query).fetch_all(&self.pool).await?;

        Ok(rows.into_iter().map(|row| AgentMemory {
            id: row.get(0), origin: row.get(1), directive: row.get(2), result: row.get(3), timestamp: row.get(4),
        }).collect())
    }

    pub async fn semantic_search(&self, query_vec: &[f32]) -> Result<Vec<AgentMemory>, sqlx::Error> {
        let rows = sqlx::query("SELECT id, origin, directive, result, timestamp, embedding FROM neural_context WHERE embedding IS NOT NULL")
            .fetch_all(&self.pool).await?;

        let mut scored_mems = Vec::new();
        for row in rows {
            let emb_str: Option<String> = row.get("embedding");
            if let Some(s) = emb_str {
                if let Ok(vec) = serde_json::from_str::<Vec<f32>>(&s) {
                    let score = crate::vector_bridge::cosine_similarity(query_vec, &vec);
                    scored_mems.push((score, row));
                }
            }
        }

        scored_mems.sort_by(|a, b| b.0.partial_cmp(&a.0).unwrap_or(std::cmp::Ordering::Equal));
        scored_mems.truncate(3); 

        Ok(scored_mems.into_iter().map(|(_, row)| AgentMemory {
            id: row.get("id"), origin: row.get("origin"), directive: row.get("directive"),
            result: row.get("result"), timestamp: row.get("timestamp"),
        }).collect())
    }

    pub async fn purge_all(&self) -> Result<(), sqlx::Error> {
        sqlx::query("DELETE FROM neural_context").execute(&self.pool).await?;
        sqlx::query("DELETE FROM memory_search").execute(&self.pool).await?;
        sqlx::query("DELETE FROM system_insights").execute(&self.pool).await?;
        Ok(())
    }

    pub async fn fetch_random_strands(&self, limit: i64) -> Result<Vec<AgentMemory>, sqlx::Error> {
        let rows = sqlx::query("SELECT id, origin, directive, result, timestamp FROM neural_context ORDER BY RANDOM() LIMIT ?")
            .bind(limit).fetch_all(&self.pool).await?;
        Ok(rows.into_iter().map(|row| AgentMemory {
            id: row.get("id"), origin: row.get("origin"), directive: row.get("directive"), result: row.get("result"), timestamp: row.get("timestamp"),
        }).collect())
    }
}
</FILE>

<FILE path="crates/nexus_substrate/build.rs">
//! build.rs — nexus_substrate v3.0
//! Uses `zig build-lib` directly — no build.zig needed.
//! This is the fastest path on ARM/eMMC hardware (Lenovo Duet).

use std::process::Command;
use std::env;

fn main() {
    let out_dir      = env::var("OUT_DIR").unwrap();
    let manifest_dir = env::current_dir().unwrap();
    let zig_src      = manifest_dir.join("src/kernel/muscle.zig");

    println!("cargo:rerun-if-changed=src/kernel/muscle.zig");

    // Derive target triple from Cargo's env vars
    let arch = env::var("CARGO_CFG_TARGET_ARCH").unwrap_or_default();
    let os   = env::var("CARGO_CFG_TARGET_OS").unwrap_or_default();
    let zig_target = match (arch.as_str(), os.as_str()) {
        ("aarch64", "linux") => "aarch64-linux-musl",
        ("x86_64",  "linux") => "x86_64-linux-musl",
        _                    => "native",
    };

    let output = Command::new("zig")
        .args([
            "build-lib",
            "-target", zig_target,
            "-O", "ReleaseFast",
            "-lc",
            "-fcompiler-rt",
            zig_src.to_str().unwrap(),
            &format!("-femit-bin={}/libmuscle.a", out_dir),
            "--name", "muscle",
            "-static",
        ])
        .output()
        .unwrap_or_else(|e| {
            panic!(
                "[build.rs] Could not launch `zig`: {}\n\
                 Install zig >= 0.15.2 and ensure it is on PATH.",
                e
            )
        });

    if !output.stdout.is_empty() {
        eprint!("{}", String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stdout));
    }
    if !output.stderr.is_empty() {
        eprint!("{}", String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stderr));
    }

    if !output.status.success() {
        panic!(
            "[build.rs] Zig compilation failed (exit: {}).\n\
             Source: {}\n\
             See stderr above.",
            output.status,
            zig_src.display()
        );
    }

    println!("cargo:rustc-link-search=native={}", out_dir);
    println!("cargo:rustc-link-lib=static=muscle");
}
</FILE>

use crate::agents::{LogicAgent, agentic_runtime::SubstrateContext};
use anyhow::Result;
use async_trait::async_trait;

pub struct ScoutAgent;

impl ScoutAgent {
    pub fn new() -> Self { Self }
}

#[async_trait]
impl LogicAgent for ScoutAgent {
    fn name(&self) -> String { "ScoutAgent".to_string() }
    
    fn can_handle(&self, command: &str) -> bool {
        let c = command.to_lowercase();
        c.starts_with("ls") || c.starts_with("find") || c.starts_with("grep")
    }

    async fn execute(&self, command: &str, _ctx: &SubstrateContext) -> Result<String> {
        let out = crate::podman::PodmanShield::execute(command)?;
        Ok(String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stdout).into_owned())
    }
}

<FILE path="crates/runtime_core/src/agents/agentic_runtime.rs">
use std::sync::Arc;
use tokio::sync::Mutex;
use anyhow::Result;
use crate::agents::LogicAgent;
use crate::shared_state::VmoState;
use crate::distributed_scheduler::DistributedScheduler;
use nexus_substrate::memory::MemoryPalace;

pub struct SubstrateContext {
    pub memory: Arc<Mutex<MemoryPalace>>,
    pub vmo_state: Arc<Mutex<VmoState>>,
    pub dist: Arc<DistributedScheduler>,
}

pub struct AgenticRuntime {
    pub ctx: SubstrateContext,
    pub agents: Vec<Box<dyn LogicAgent>>,
}

impl AgenticRuntime {
    pub fn new(ctx: SubstrateContext) -> Self {
        Self {
            ctx,
            agents: Vec::new(),
        }
    }

    pub fn register_agent(&mut self, agent: Box<dyn LogicAgent>) {
        self.agents.push(agent);
    }

    pub async fn dispatch(&mut self, command: &str) -> Result<String> {
        // Search for a specialized agent
        for agent in &self.agents {
            if agent.can_handle(command) {
                return agent.execute(command, &self.ctx).await;
            }
        }

        // Fallback to basic shell execution if no specialized agent matches
        let output = crate::podman::PodmanShield::execute(command)
            .map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!("Substrate Execution Fault: {}", e))?;
        
        let stdout = String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stdout).to_string();
        let stderr = String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stderr).to_string();
        
        if !output.status.success() && stdout.is_empty() {
            Ok(format!("[ERROR] {}", stderr))
        } else {
            Ok(stdout)
        }
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::cluster::ClusterMesh;
    
    #[tokio::test]
    async fn test_runtime_init() {
        // This test only verifies that the structures can be initialized in memory
        let mesh = Arc::new(ClusterMesh::new());
        let dist = Arc::new(DistributedScheduler::new(mesh));
        // Note: We don't need a real DB for a basic logic check
        println!("LRL Structural Integrity: OK");
    }
}

<FILE path="crates/runtime_core/src/agents/scribe_agent.rs">
use crate::agent_protocol::{AgentDirective, AgentStatus};
use crate::agents::NexusAgent;
use nexus_substrate::kernel;
use anyhow::Result;
use async_trait::async_trait;

pub struct ScribeAgent;

impl ScribeAgent {
    pub fn enshrine(handle: u32, offset: usize, data: &str) -> bool {
        let bytes = data.as_bytes();
        unsafe {
            kernel::ffi::zx_vmo_write_native(handle, offset, bytes.as_ptr(), bytes.len()) == 0
        }
    }

    pub fn recall(handle: u32, offset: usize, size: usize) -> Option<String> {
        let mut buffer = vec![0u8; size];
        unsafe {
            if kernel::ffi::zx_vmo_read_native(handle, offset, buffer.as_mut_ptr(), size) == 0 {
                String::from_utf8(buffer).ok()
            } else {
                None
            }
        }
    }

    pub fn scry_vault(handle: u32, offset: usize, size: usize) {
        let mut buffer = vec![0u8; size];
        unsafe {
            if kernel::ffi::zx_vmo_read_native(handle, offset, buffer.as_mut_ptr(), size) == 0 {
                println!("\x1b[1;30m--- [SCRY: 0x{:x} | Offset: {}] ---\x1b[0m", handle, offset);
                for chunk in buffer.chunks(16) {
                    let hex: String = chunk.iter().map(|b| format!("{:02x} ", b)).collect();
                    let ascii: String = chunk
                        .iter()
                        .map(|&b| if b >= 32 && b <= 126 { b as char } else { '.' })
                        .collect();
                    println!("\x1b[35m{:32} \x1b[1;37m| {}\x1b[0m", hex, ascii);
                }
            } else {
                println!(
                    "\x1b[31m[SCRY] Access Denied: Handle 0x{:x} is opaque or invalid.\x1b[0m",
                    handle
                );
            }
        }
    }
}

#[async_trait]
impl NexusAgent for ScribeAgent {
    fn name(&self) -> &'static str {
        "scribe_io_expert"
    }
    async fn status(&self) -> AgentStatus {
        AgentStatus::Idle
    }
    async fn execute(&self, _directive: AgentDirective) -> Result<String> {
        Ok("Scribe standing by for commutation.".to_string())
    }
}

use nexus_substrate::kernel;

pub struct DataAgent {
    pub id: String,
    pub vmo_handle: Option<u32>,
}

impl DataAgent {
    pub fn new(id: &str) -> Self {
        Self { id: id.to_string(), vmo_handle: None }
    }

    /// Commands the muscle to carve out a 64KB sovereign vault
    pub fn initialize_vault(&mut self) {
        let size = 64 * 1024; // 64KB agent vault
        let handle = unsafe { kernel::ffi::zx_vmo_create_native(size) };
        kernel::register_handle(handle, kernel::HandleType::Vmo(handle));
        self.vmo_handle = Some(handle);
        println!("\x1b[35m[DATA_AGENT:{}] Vault carved at handle 0x{:x}\x1b[0m", self.id, handle);
    }
}

use bitflags::bitflags;

bitflags! {
    #[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, PartialOrd, Ord, Hash)]
    pub struct Capabilities: u32 {
        const NONE     = 0;
        const READ     = 1 << 0;
        const WRITE    = 1 << 1;
        const EXEC     = 1 << 2;
        const NETWORK  = 1 << 3;
    }
}

pub struct Sandbox {
    caps: Capabilities,
}

impl Sandbox {
    pub fn new(caps: Capabilities) -> Self { Self { caps } }
    pub fn can_read(&self) -> bool { self.caps.contains(Capabilities::READ) }
    pub fn can_write(&self) -> bool { self.caps.contains(Capabilities::WRITE) }
    pub fn can_exec(&self) -> bool { self.caps.contains(Capabilities::EXEC) }
    pub fn can_network(&self) -> bool { self.caps.contains(Capabilities::NETWORK) }
}

<FILE path="crates/runtime_core/src/orchestrator_v2/task_graph.rs">
use std::collections::{HashMap, VecDeque};
use anyhow::{anyhow, Result};

#[derive(Clone)]
pub struct TaskNode {
    pub id: String,
    pub deps: Vec<String>,
    pub required_agent: String,
}

pub struct TaskGraph {
    pub nodes: HashMap<String, TaskNode>,
}

impl TaskGraph {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self { nodes: HashMap::new() }
    }

    pub fn add_task(&mut self, id: &str, deps: Vec<String>, required_agent: &str) {
        self.nodes.insert(id.to_string(), TaskNode {
            id: id.to_string(),
            deps,
            required_agent: required_agent.to_string(),
        });
    }

    pub fn resolve_execution_order(&self) -> Result<Vec<String>> {
        let mut resolved = Vec::new();
        let mut queue: VecDeque<_> = self.nodes.values().cloned().collect();
        
        let mut iterations = 0;
        let max_iterations = self.nodes.len() * self.nodes.len() + 1;

        while let Some(node) = queue.pop_front() {
            iterations += 1;
            
            // ⚡ DEADLOCK TRAP FIXED
            if iterations > max_iterations {
                return Err(anyhow!("DAG_DEADLOCK: Circular dependency detected. Aborting."));
            }

            if node.deps.iter().all(|d| resolved.contains(d)) {
                resolved.push(node.id.clone());
            } else {
                queue.push_back(node);
            }
        }
        Ok(resolved)
    }
}
</FILE>

<FILE path="crates/runtime_core/src/orchestrator_v2/mod.rs">
pub mod task_graph;

use crate::agent_runtime::engine::{Task, TaskResult, ExecutableAgent};
use self::task_graph::TaskGraph;
use std::collections::HashMap;
use anyhow::{anyhow, Result};

pub struct OrchestratorV2 {
    // ⚡ DYNAMIC DISPATCH: Supports a diverse swarm of agents simultaneously
    agents: HashMap<String, Box<dyn ExecutableAgent>>, 
}

impl OrchestratorV2 {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self { agents: HashMap::new() }
    }

    pub fn register_agent(&mut self, name: &str, agent: Box<dyn ExecutableAgent>) {
        self.agents.insert(name.to_string(), agent);
    }

    pub async fn execute_graph(&self, graph: TaskGraph) -> Result<Vec<TaskResult>> {
        let order = graph.resolve_execution_order()?;
        let mut results = Vec::new();

        for id in order {
            let node = graph.nodes.get(&id).unwrap();
            
            let agent = self.agents.get(&node.required_agent)
                .ok_or_else(|| anyhow!("AGENT_MISSING: '{}' not registered in swarm.", node.required_agent))?;

            let task = Task {
                id: id.clone(),
                payload: format!("Execute directive for {}", id),
            };

            let result = agent.execute(task).await?;
            results.push(result);
        }

        Ok(results)
    }
}
</FILE>

<FILE path="crates/runtime_core/src/governance/mod.rs">
// governance/mod.rs — v2.0 [MERKLE_CHAIN]
// Incremental Merkle root: each action hashes into a running chain.
// Provides a tamper-evident audit trail of every agent action.
pub mod protocol;
</FILE>

<FILE path="crates/runtime_core/src/governance/protocol.rs">
// governance/protocol.rs — v2.0 [MERKLE_CHAIN]
use crate::agent_protocol::AgentAction;
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use std::collections::hash_map::DefaultHasher;
use std::hash::{Hash, Hasher};
use std::time::{SystemTime, UNIX_EPOCH};
use tracing::info;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct MerkleLeaf {
    pub action:    AgentAction,
    pub actor_id:  String,
    pub timestamp: u64,
    pub hash:      u64,
}

impl MerkleLeaf {
    pub fn new(action: AgentAction, actor_id: &str) -> Self {
        let ts = SystemTime::now()
            .duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH)
            .unwrap_or_default()
            .as_secs();
        let mut hasher = DefaultHasher::new();
        serde_json::to_vec(&action).unwrap_or_default().hash(&mut hasher);
        actor_id.hash(&mut hasher);
        ts.hash(&mut hasher);
        Self {
            action,
            actor_id: actor_id.to_string(),
            timestamp: ts,
            hash: hasher.finish(),
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, Default)]
pub struct GovernanceLog {
    pub history:   Vec<MerkleLeaf>,
    pub root_hash: u64,
}

impl GovernanceLog {
    pub fn init() -> Self {
        Self::default()
    }

    /// record: Chains a new leaf into the Merkle root.
    /// Root = Hash(OldRoot ++ NewLeaf)
    pub fn record(&mut self, action: AgentAction, actor_id: &str) {
        let leaf = MerkleLeaf::new(action, actor_id);

        let mut hasher = DefaultHasher::new();
        self.root_hash.hash(&mut hasher);
        leaf.hash.hash(&mut hasher);
        self.root_hash = hasher.finish();

        self.history.push(leaf);

        if self.history.len() % 10 == 0 {
            info!(
                "\x1b[34m[GOVERNANCE] Checkpoint: Root=0x{:x} (Size: {})\x1b[0m",
                self.root_hash,
                self.history.len()
            );
        }
    }

    pub fn root(&self) -> u64 {
        self.root_hash
    }

    pub fn len(&self) -> usize {
        self.history.len()
    }

    pub fn is_empty(&self) -> bool {
        self.history.is_empty()
    }
}
</FILE>

<FILE path="crates/runtime_core/src/v5_engine/capabilities/mod.rs">
use serde::{Serialize, Deserialize};

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Hash, Eq, PartialEq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub enum Capability {
    ReadFile,
    WriteFile,
    ExecuteProcess,
    NetworkAccess,
    MemoryModify,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct IsolationBoundary {
    pub allowed_capabilities: std::collections::HashSet<Capability>,
    pub run_as_wasm: bool,
}

impl IsolationBoundary {
    pub fn enforce(&self, intent: &Capability) -> Result<(), String> {
        if !self.allowed_capabilities.contains(intent) {
            return Err(format!("SECURITY FAULT: Capability {:?} denied by sandbox isolation.", intent));
        }
        Ok(())
    }
}
</FILE>

<FILE path="crates/runtime_core/src/v5_engine/graph/mod.rs">
use std::collections::{HashMap, VecDeque};

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct ExecutionNode {
    pub id: String,
    pub deps: Vec<String>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Default)]
pub struct ExecutionGraph {
    pub nodes: HashMap<String, ExecutionNode>,
}

impl ExecutionGraph {
    pub fn validate_and_sort(&self) -> Result<Vec<String>, String> {
        let mut indegree: HashMap<String, usize> = HashMap::new();
        let mut sorted = Vec::new();

        // Initialize indegrees
        for (id, node) in &self.nodes {
            indegree.entry(id.clone()).or_insert(0);
            for dep in &node.deps {
                *indegree.entry(dep.clone()).or_insert(0) += 1;
            }
        }

        let mut queue = VecDeque::new();
        for (node, degree) in &indegree {
            if *degree == 0 {
                queue.push_back(node.clone());
            }
        }

        let mut visited = 0;
        while let Some(node) = queue.pop_front() {
            visited += 1;
            sorted.push(node.clone());

            if let Some(n) = self.nodes.get(&node) {
                for dep in &n.deps {
                    if let Some(v) = indegree.get_mut(dep) {
                        *v -= 1;
                        if *v == 0 {
                            queue.push_back(dep.clone());
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        if visited != self.nodes.len() {
            return Err("FRACTURE: Cyclic dependency detected in DAG. Execution aborted.".into());
        }

        // Reverse to get execution order (dependencies must run first)
        sorted.reverse();
        Ok(sorted)
    }
}
</FILE>

<FILE path="crates/runtime_core/src/v5_engine/actor.rs">
use tokio::sync::{mpsc, oneshot};
use sqlx::{sqlite::SqliteConnectOptions, SqlitePool};
use std::str::FromStr;

pub enum MemoryCommand {
    CommitEvent {
        kind: String,
        object_id: String,
        respond_to: oneshot::Sender<bool>,
    },
    CommitArtifact {
        task_id: String,
        artifact_type: String,
        payload: Vec<u8>,
        respond_to: oneshot::Sender<bool>,
    },
}

pub struct MemoryActor {
    rx: mpsc::Receiver<MemoryCommand>,
    pool: SqlitePool,
}

impl MemoryActor {
    pub async fn ignite(db_url: &str) -> Result<mpsc::Sender<MemoryCommand>, sqlx::Error> {
        // Enforce WAL mode for ARM64 IO performance
        let options = SqliteConnectOptions::from_str(db_url)?
            .create_if_missing(true)
            .journal_mode(sqlx::sqlite::SqliteJournalMode::Wal)
            .synchronous(sqlx::sqlite::SqliteSynchronous::Normal);

        let pool = SqlitePool::connect_with(options).await?;

        // MSK Core Schema
        sqlx::query("
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS tasks (
                id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                status TEXT NOT NULL,
                objective TEXT NOT NULL,
                created_at INTEGER NOT NULL
            );
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS events (
                seq INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                kind TEXT NOT NULL,
                object_id TEXT NOT NULL,
                ts INTEGER NOT NULL
            );
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS artifacts (
                id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                task_id TEXT NOT NULL,
                artifact_type TEXT NOT NULL,
                payload BLOB NOT NULL
            );
        ").execute(&pool).await?;

        // Bound the queue to 64 to enforce backpressure
        let (tx, rx) = mpsc::channel(64);
        
        let actor = Self { rx, pool };
        
        tokio::spawn(async move {
            actor.run_loop().await;
        });
        
        println!("\x1b[32m>> [ACTOR] MSK SQLite WAL Substrate Online. Single-writer active.\x1b[0m");
        Ok(tx)
    }

    async fn run_loop(mut self) {
        while let Some(cmd) = self.rx.recv().await {
            match cmd {
                MemoryCommand::CommitEvent { kind, object_id, respond_to } => {
                    let ts = std::time::SystemTime::now().duration_since(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH).unwrap().as_secs() as i64;
                    let res = sqlx::query("INSERT INTO events (kind, object_id, ts) VALUES (?, ?, ?)")
                        .bind(kind).bind(object_id).bind(ts)
                        .execute(&self.pool).await.is_ok();
                    let _ = respond_to.send(res);
                }
                MemoryCommand::CommitArtifact { task_id, artifact_type, payload, respond_to } => {
                    let id = format!("{}_{}", task_id, artifact_type); // Simple compound ID
                    let res = sqlx::query("INSERT INTO artifacts (id, task_id, artifact_type, payload) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)")
                        .bind(id).bind(task_id).bind(artifact_type).bind(payload)
                        .execute(&self.pool).await.is_ok();
                    let _ = respond_to.send(res);
                }
            }
        }
    }
}
</FILE>

use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

#[derive(Debug, Serialize, Deserialize, Clone, PartialEq)]

pub struct AgentDirective {
    pub id: Uuid,
    pub task_type: AgentTaskType,
    pub payload: String,
    pub priority: u8,

#[derive(Debug, Serialize, Deserialize, Clone, PartialEq)]
#[serde(tag = "type")]
pub enum AgentAction {
    #[serde(rename = "execute_command")] ExecuteCommand { command: String, #[serde(default)] reasoning: String },
    #[serde(rename = "read_file")] ReadFile { path: String, #[serde(default)] reasoning: String },
    #[serde(rename = "write_file")] WriteFile { path: String, content: String, #[serde(default)] reasoning: String },
    #[serde(rename = "search_memory")] SearchMemory { query: String, #[serde(default)] reasoning: String },
    #[serde(rename = "vmo_create")] VmoCreate { size: usize, #[serde(default)] reasoning: String },
    #[serde(rename = "calculate_entropy")] CalculateEntropy { handle: String, #[serde(default)] reasoning: String },
    #[serde(rename = "lazarus_snapshot")] LazarusSnapshot { path: String, tag: Option<String>, #[serde(default)] reasoning: String },
    #[serde(rename = "export_report")] ExportReport { title: String, content: String, #[serde(default)] reasoning: String },
    #[serde(rename = "fetch_ui_snapshot")] FetchUiSnapshot { #[serde(default)] reasoning: String },
    #[serde(rename = "patch_function")] PatchFunction { path: String, function_name: String, new_body: String, #[serde(default)] reasoning: String },
    #[serde(rename = "scrape_url")] ScrapeUrl { url: String, #[serde(default)] reasoning: String },
    #[serde(rename = "halt")] Halt { #[serde(default)] reasoning: String },
    #[serde(rename = "diff_patch")] DiffPatch { path: String, search: String, replace: String, #[serde(default)] reasoning: String },
}

<FILE path="crates/runtime_core/src/json_parser.rs">
use anyhow::{anyhow, Result};
use crate::agent_protocol::AgentAction;
use serde_json::Value;

/// Robust extractor that handles most LLM output variations
pub fn extract_actions(response: &str) -> Result<Vec<AgentAction>> {
    let text = response.trim();

    // Strategy 1: Direct parse (clean JSON)
    if let Ok(actions) = serde_json::from_str::<Vec<AgentAction>>(text) {
        if !actions.is_empty() {
            return Ok(actions);
        }
    }

    // Strategy 2: Extract largest JSON array (handles markdown, extra text, thoughts)
    if let Some(array_str) = extract_largest_json_array(text) {
        if let Ok(actions) = serde_json::from_str::<Vec<AgentAction>>(&array_str) {
            if !actions.is_empty() {
                return Ok(actions);
            }
        }
    }

    // Strategy 3: Single object fallback
    if let Ok(obj) = serde_json::from_str::<Value>(text) {
        if obj.is_object() {
            if let Ok(action) = serde_json::from_value::<AgentAction>(obj) {
                return Ok(vec![action]);
            }
        }
    }

    // Strategy 4: Aggressive normalization + legacy field support
    let normalized = normalize_action_json(text);
    if let Ok(actions) = serde_json::from_str::<Vec<AgentAction>>(&normalized) {
        if !actions.is_empty() {
            return Ok(actions);
        }
    }

    Err(anyhow!(
        "PARSER_FRACTURE: Could not extract valid actions after multiple strategies.\nPreview: {:.200}",
        text
    ))
}

fn extract_largest_json_array(text: &str) -> Option<String> {
    let mut best_start = 0;
    let mut best_end = 0;
    let mut max_len = 0;

    let mut i = 0;
    while let Some(start) = text[i..].find('[') {
        let abs_start = i + start;
        let mut depth = 0;
        let mut end = None;

        for (j, c) in text[abs_start..].char_indices() {
            match c {
                '[' => depth += 1,
                ']' => {
                    depth -= 1;
                    if depth == 0 {
                        end = Some(abs_start + j + 1);
                        break;
                    }
                }
                _ => {}
            }
        }

        if let Some(e) = end {
            let len = e - abs_start;
            if len > max_len {
                max_len = len;
                best_start = abs_start;
                best_end = e;
            }
            i = e;
        } else {
            break;
        }
    }

    if max_len > 10 {
        Some(text[best_start..best_end].to_string())
    } else {
        None
    }
}

fn normalize_action_json(text: &str) -> String {
    let mut s = text
        .replace("```json", "")
        .replace("```", "")
        .replace("action:", "\"type\":")
        .replace("\"action\"", "\"type\"")
        .trim()
        .to_string();

    // If it starts with { but is not wrapped in [], make it an array
    if s.starts_with('{') && !s.starts_with('[') {
        s = format!("[{}]", s);
    }
    s
}
</FILE>

pub struct AgentFactory;

            "security" => "IDENTITY: NEXUS Security Auditor. ROLE: Identify logic flaws, path traversal, unsafe unwraps, and auth gaps. OUTPUT: ONLY a valid JSON array. No prose.".to_string(),
            "docs" => "IDENTITY: NEXUS Archivist. ROLE: Generate technical markdown, README files, and architecture maps. OUTPUT: ONLY a valid JSON array of write_file actions.".to_string(),

        }

<FILE path="crates/runtime_core/src/conductor.rs">
use crate::metrics::METRICS;
use crate::neural_agent::ContextPacket;
use crate::orchestrator::Orchestrator;
use crate::router::AgentProfile;
use nexus_substrate::token_ledger::wait_for_quota;
use reqwest::Client;
use serde_json::json;
use std::sync::atomic::Ordering;
use tokio::sync::broadcast;

const ESTIMATED_TOKENS: u32 = 2_000;

pub struct Conductor;

impl Conductor {
    pub async fn run_turn(
        profile: &AgentProfile,
        prompt: String,
        turn: usize,
        orch: &Orchestrator,
        client: &Client,
        api_key: &str,
        tx: Option<broadcast::Sender<String>>,
    ) -> Result<String, String> {
        METRICS.api_calls.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);

        let model: &str = if turn > 6 { "models/gemma-4-31b-it" } else { "models/gemma-4-31b-it" };
        wait_for_quota(model, ESTIMATED_TOKENS).await;

        let ctx = ContextPacket {
            system_prompt: Some(profile.persona.clone()),
            model_override: Some(model.to_string()),
            ..Default::default()
        };

        if let Some(ref sender) = tx {
            let _ = sender.send(
                json!({"type": "NEURAL_THOUGHT", "payload": format!("[CONDUCTOR] turn {} → {}", turn, model)}).to_string()
            );
        }

        let result = orch.run(prompt.clone(), ctx, client, api_key, tx.clone()).await;

        if let Err(ref e) = result {
            if e.contains("STALE") || e.contains("500") || e.contains("RESOURCE_EXHAUSTED") || e.contains("INTERNAL") || e.contains("CONTEXT_OVERFLOW") {
                METRICS.task_failures.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
                
                let failover: &str = if model == "models/gemma-4-31b-it" { "models/gemma-4-31b-it" } else { "models/gemma-4-31b-it" };
                wait_for_quota(failover, ESTIMATED_TOKENS).await;

                let fail_ctx = ContextPacket {
                    system_prompt: Some(profile.persona.clone()),
                    model_override: Some(failover.to_string()),
                    ..Default::default()
                };
                return orch.run(prompt, fail_ctx, client, api_key, tx).await;
            }
        }
        result
    }
}
</FILE>

<FILE path="crates/runtime_core/src/router.rs">
// src/router.rs — Intelligent Agent Router v4.1 [REPO_DOCTOR_ACTIVE]
// Logic: Task-specific budget allocation with strict JSON enforcement.

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub enum DirectiveClass {
    ShellOp,
    TargetedEdit { file_hint: String },
    RepoDoctor, // ⚡ NEW: Automated Workspace Healing
    SecurityAudit,
    WasmForge, 
    Inquiry,
    FeatureBuild,
    General,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct ContextBudget {
    pub max_file_chars: usize,
    pub max_obs_lines: usize,
    pub max_schema_chars: usize,
    pub max_output_tokens: u32,
}

impl ContextBudget {
    pub fn minimal() -> Self {
        Self { max_file_chars: 1_500, max_obs_lines: 2, max_schema_chars: 400, max_output_tokens: 1024 }
    }
    pub fn standard() -> Self {
        Self { max_file_chars: 8_000, max_obs_lines: 6, max_schema_chars: 1_200, max_output_tokens: 4096 }
    }
    pub fn doctor() -> Self {
        Self { max_file_chars: 15_000, max_obs_lines: 10, max_schema_chars: 1_500, max_output_tokens: 8192 }
    }
    pub fn security() -> Self {
        Self { max_file_chars: 20_000, max_obs_lines: 10, max_schema_chars: 2_000, max_output_tokens: 8192 }
    }
}

pub struct AgentProfile {
    pub class: DirectiveClass,
    pub primary_model: &'static str,
    pub max_turns: u8,
    pub budget: ContextBudget,
    pub persona: String,
}

pub struct IntelligentRouter;

impl IntelligentRouter {
    pub fn classify(actual_directive: &str) -> DirectiveClass {
        let d = actual_directive.to_lowercase();

        if ["diagnose", "doctor", "fix repo", "repair workspace", "suture"].iter().any(|k| d.contains(k)) {
            return DirectiveClass::RepoDoctor;
        }
        if ["synthesize", "forge", "wasm cell"].iter().any(|k| d.contains(k)) {
            return DirectiveClass::WasmForge;
        }
        if ["cargo check", "cargo test", "git status", "ls ", "find "].iter().any(|k| d.contains(k)) {
            return DirectiveClass::ShellOp;
        }
        if ["audit ", "vulnerability", "memory safe"].iter().any(|k| d.contains(k)) {
            return DirectiveClass::SecurityAudit;
        }
        if ["fix", "patch", "update", "edit"].iter().any(|k| d.contains(k)) {
            return DirectiveClass::TargetedEdit { file_hint: "target".into() };
        }
        DirectiveClass::General
    }

    pub fn route(directive: &str) -> AgentProfile {
        let start_idx = directive.find("DIRECTIVE: ").map(|i| i + 11).unwrap_or(0);
        let user_cmd = &directive[start_idx..];

        let class = Self::classify(user_cmd);

        match class {
            DirectiveClass::RepoDoctor => AgentProfile {
                class: DirectiveClass::RepoDoctor,
                primary_model: "models/gemma-4-31b-it", // ⚡ High-reasoning model
                max_turns: 8,
                budget: ContextBudget::doctor(),
                persona: "IDENTITY: NEXUS Repo Doctor. Goal: Diagnose and patch compilation errors. RULE: You MUST output ONLY a valid JSON array. WORKFLOW: 1. read_file on the failing file. 2. write_file with the corrected code. 3. execute_command with 'cargo check' to verify. 4. halt only when integrity passes.".into(),
            },
            DirectiveClass::WasmForge => AgentProfile {
                class: DirectiveClass::WasmForge,
                primary_model: "models/gemma-4-31b-it",
                max_turns: 5,
                budget: ContextBudget::standard(),
                persona: "IDENTITY: NEXUS Forge Master. Goal: Synthesize Rust WASM cells. RULE: You MUST output a valid JSON array inside an <ACTION> block containing {\"type\": \"synthesize_wasm\", \"code\": \"...\"}. DO NOT write markdown tutorials. DO NOT output WAT format. RULE 2: You MUST execute {\"type\": \"halt\"} immediately after a successful synthesis.".into(),
            },
            DirectiveClass::ShellOp => AgentProfile {
                class: DirectiveClass::ShellOp,
                primary_model: "models/gemma-4-31b-it",
                max_turns: 6,
                budget: ContextBudget::minimal(),
                persona: "IDENTITY: NEXUS DevOps. Goal: Substrate maintenance. RULE: You MUST execute operations using ONLY a valid JSON array where every object has a 'type' field.".into(),
            },
            _ => AgentProfile {
                class: DirectiveClass::General,
                primary_model: "models/gemma-4-31b-it",
                max_turns: 8,
                budget: ContextBudget::standard(),
                persona: "IDENTITY: NEXUS Sovereign Agent. Goal: Fulfill the directive. RULE: You MUST execute operations using ONLY a valid JSON array where every object has a 'type' field. NEVER output conversational text. Output ONLY JSON.".into(),
            },
        }
    }

    pub fn build_prompt(directive: &str, profile: &AgentProfile, obs: &[String]) -> String {
        let history = obs.iter().map(|o| format!(">> {}", o)).collect::<Vec<_>>().join("\n");
        format!(
            "{}\n\nMISSION HISTORY:\n{}\n\nCURRENT OBJECTIVE: {}\n\nNEXT STEP (Output ONLY a valid JSON array):",
            profile.persona, history, directive
        )
    }
}
</FILE>

<FILE path="crates/runtime_core/src/logic_engine.rs">
use std::collections::HashMap;
use tracing::warn;

pub struct LogicEngine;

impl LogicEngine {
    pub fn is_action_safe(directive: &str) -> bool {
        let forbidden = ["rm -rf /", "mkfs", "shutdown", "chmod 777", "dd if=/dev/zero", "> /dev/sda"];
        let d_lower = directive.to_lowercase();
        !forbidden.iter().any(|&p| d_lower.contains(p))
    }

    pub fn detect_deadlock(observations: &[String]) -> bool {
        if observations.len() < 3 { return false; }

        let mut read_counts: HashMap<String, usize> = HashMap::new();

        for obs in observations {
            if let Some(after) = obs.strip_prefix("[READ_OK] ") {
                let path = after.split_whitespace().next().unwrap_or("").to_string();
                if !path.is_empty() {
                    *read_counts.entry(path).or_insert(0) += 1;
                }
            }
        }

        for (path, count) in read_counts {
            if count >= 3 {
                let file_progress = observations.iter().any(|obs| {
                    (obs.contains("[WRITE_OK]") || obs.contains("[DIFF_PATCH_OK]") || obs.contains("[PATCH_OK]"))
                        && obs.contains(&path)
                });

                if !file_progress {
                    warn!("[LOGIC] Localized Deadlock: {} read {} times with zero progress.", path, count);
                    return true;
                }
            }
        }

        let mut last_cmd_sig = String::new();
        let mut cmd_repeat_count = 0;

        for obs in observations {
            if let Some(start) = obs.find("[CMD:") {
                let sig: String = obs[start..]
                    .lines()
                    .filter(|line| !line.contains("Finished") && !line.contains("Blocking waiting"))
                    .collect::<Vec<&str>>()
                    .join("\n");

                if sig == last_cmd_sig && !sig.is_empty() {
                    cmd_repeat_count += 1;
                } else {
                    last_cmd_sig = sig;
                    cmd_repeat_count = 1;
                }
            }
        }

        if cmd_repeat_count >= 2 {
            warn!("[LOGIC] Deadlock: Time-invariant command repetition detected.");
            return true;
        }
        false
    }
}
</FILE>

<FILE path="crates/runtime_core/src/metrics.rs">
/**
 * src/metrics.rs — v4.1 Atomic Sovereign
 * Copyright 2026 Donald Ball
 * Lightweight, lock-free, zero-dependency metrics using only std atomics.
 * Fully compatible with Prometheus scraping.
 */

use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicU64, Ordering};
use std::sync::LazyLock;

#[derive(Debug)]
pub struct NexusMetrics {
    pub tasks_total:       AtomicU64,
    pub task_failures:     AtomicU64,
    pub arbiter_blocks:    AtomicU64,
    pub heal_successes:    AtomicU64,
    pub heal_failures:     AtomicU64,
    pub api_latency_ms_sum: AtomicU64,
    pub api_calls:         AtomicU64,
    pub circuit_opens:     AtomicU64,
}

impl NexusMetrics {
    const fn new() -> Self {
        Self {
            tasks_total: AtomicU64::new(0),
            task_failures: AtomicU64::new(0),
            arbiter_blocks: AtomicU64::new(0),
            heal_successes: AtomicU64::new(0),
            heal_failures: AtomicU64::new(0),
            api_latency_ms_sum: AtomicU64::new(0),
            api_calls: AtomicU64::new(0),
            circuit_opens: AtomicU64::new(0),
        }
    }

    /// Increment total tasks processed
    pub fn inc_task(&self) {
        self.tasks_total.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
    }

    /// Increment task failures
    pub fn inc_failure(&self) {
        self.task_failures.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
    }

    /// Increment arbiter blocks (safety interventions)
    pub fn inc_arbiter_block(&self) {
        self.arbiter_blocks.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
    }

    /// Increment successful self-healing sutures
    pub fn inc_heal_success(&self) {
        self.heal_successes.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
    }

    /// Increment failed self-healing attempts
    pub fn inc_heal_failure(&self) {
        self.heal_failures.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
    }

    /// Increment circuit breaker trips
    pub fn inc_circuit_open(&self) {
        self.circuit_opens.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
    }

    /// Record API call with latency (used by Orchestrator)
    pub fn record_api_call(&self, latency_ms: u128) {
        self.api_calls.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
        self.api_latency_ms_sum.fetch_add(latency_ms as u64, Ordering::Relaxed);
    }

    /// Compute average latency (integer ms)
    pub fn avg_latency_ms(&self) -> u64 {
        let calls = self.api_calls.load(Ordering::Relaxed);
        if calls == 0 {
            0
        } else {
            self.api_latency_ms_sum.load(Ordering::Relaxed) / calls
        }
    }
}

pub static METRICS: LazyLock<NexusMetrics> = LazyLock::new(NexusMetrics::new);

/// Returns full Prometheus exposition format text (ready for scraping)
pub fn get_metrics_text() -> String {
    let m = &*METRICS;
    format!(
        "# HELP nexus_tasks_total Total tasks processed by the NEXUS Cortex\n\
         # TYPE nexus_tasks_total counter\n\
         nexus_tasks_total {}\n\
         # HELP nexus_task_failures Total task execution failures\n\
         # TYPE nexus_task_failures counter\n\
         nexus_task_failures {}\n\
         # HELP nexus_arbiter_blocks Total arbiter safety interventions\n\
         # TYPE nexus_arbiter_blocks counter\n\
         nexus_arbiter_blocks {}\n\
         # HELP nexus_heal_successes Self-healing suture successes\n\
         # TYPE nexus_heal_successes counter\n\
         nexus_heal_successes {}\n\
         # HELP nexus_heal_failures Self-healing suture failures\n\
         # TYPE nexus_heal_failures counter\n\
         nexus_heal_failures {}\n\
         # HELP nexus_api_calls Total calls to Gemini API backend\n\
         # TYPE nexus_api_calls counter\n\
         nexus_api_calls {}\n\
         # HELP nexus_api_avg_latency_ms Average API latency in milliseconds\n\
         # TYPE nexus_api_avg_latency_ms gauge\n\
         nexus_api_avg_latency_ms {}\n\
         # HELP nexus_circuit_opens Circuit breaker trips\n\
         # TYPE nexus_circuit_opens counter\n\
         nexus_circuit_opens {}\n",
        m.tasks_total.load(Ordering::Relaxed),
        m.task_failures.load(Ordering::Relaxed),
        m.arbiter_blocks.load(Ordering::Relaxed),
        m.heal_successes.load(Ordering::Relaxed),
        m.heal_failures.load(Ordering::Relaxed),
        m.api_calls.load(Ordering::Relaxed),

        m.circuit_opens.load(Ordering::Relaxed),
    )

<FILE path="crates/runtime_core/src/wasm_forge.rs">
use anyhow::{anyhow, Result};
use base64::{engine::general_purpose, Engine as _};
use std::process::Command;
use std::time::{SystemTime, UNIX_EPOCH};
use tokio::fs;

pub struct WasmForge;

impl WasmForge {
    pub async fn synthesize_cell(logic_code: &str) -> Result<String> {
        if logic_code.trim().starts_with("const std") || logic_code.contains("export fn") {
            return Self::synthesize_zig(logic_code).await;
        }
        Self::synthesize_rust(logic_code).await
    }

    async fn synthesize_zig(zig_code: &str) -> Result<String> {
        let ts = SystemTime::now().duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH)?.as_secs();
        let tmp_path = format!("/tmp/nexus_zig_cell_{}.zig", ts);

        fs::write(&tmp_path, zig_code).await?;

        // ⚡ ZIG 0.15 WASM COMPILATION FIX (No Linker Panics)
        let output = tokio::process::Command::new("zig")
            .args([
                "build-exe", &tmp_path,
                "-target", "wasm32-freestanding",
                "-O", "ReleaseSmall",
                "-fno-entry", "-rdynamic",
                "--name", "cell"
            ])
            .current_dir("/tmp")
            .output().await?;

        if !output.status.success() {
            let _ = fs::remove_file(&tmp_path).await;
            return Err(anyhow!("Zig compile error: {}", String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stderr)));
        }

        // Zig 0.15 writes cell.wasm to its own cwd, not /tmp
        // Try /tmp first, then fall back to current dir
        let wasm_path = if tokio::fs::metadata("/tmp/cell.wasm").await.is_ok() {
            "/tmp/cell.wasm".to_string()
        } else {
            "cell.wasm".to_string()
        };
        let wasm_bytes = fs::read(&wasm_path).await?;
        let b64 = general_purpose::STANDARD.encode(&wasm_bytes);

        let _ = fs::remove_file(&tmp_path).await;
        let _ = fs::remove_file("/tmp/cell.wasm").await;
        let _ = fs::remove_file("/tmp/cell.wasm.o").await;
        let _ = fs::remove_file("cell.wasm").await;

        Ok(b64)
    }

    async fn synthesize_rust(logic_code: &str) -> Result<String> {
        let ts = SystemTime::now().duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH)?.as_millis();
        let build_dir = format!("/tmp/nexus_forge_{}", ts);
        fs::create_dir_all(format!("{}/src", build_dir)).await?;

        let toml = r#"
[package]
name = "nexus_dynamic_cell"
version = "1.0.0"
edition = "2021"
[lib]
crate-type = ["cdylib"]
[profile.release]
opt-level = "z"
lto = true
panic = "abort"
"#;
        fs::write(format!("{}/Cargo.toml", build_dir), toml).await?;
        let wrapped_code = format!("#[no_mangle]\n{}", logic_code);
        fs::write(format!("{}/src/lib.rs", build_dir), wrapped_code).await?;

        let _ = Command::new("rustup").args(["target", "add", "wasm32-unknown-unknown"]).output();
        let output = Command::new("cargo")
            .args(["build", "--target", "wasm32-unknown-unknown", "--release"])
            .current_dir(&build_dir)
            .output()?;

        if !output.status.success() {
            let _ = fs::remove_dir_all(&build_dir).await;
            return Err(anyhow!("FORGE_FAULT: {}", String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stderr)));
        }

        let wasm_path = format!("{}/target/wasm32-unknown-unknown/release/nexus_dynamic_cell.wasm", build_dir);
        let wasm_bytes = fs::read(&wasm_path).await?;
        let base64_payload = general_purpose::STANDARD.encode(&wasm_bytes);
        let _ = fs::remove_dir_all(&build_dir).await;
        
        Ok(base64_payload)
    }
}
</FILE>

<FILE path="crates/runtime_core/src/wasm_runtime.rs">
// src/wasm_runtime.rs — v1.2.1 [LINEAR MEMORY BRIDGE + ARMOR]
use anyhow::{anyhow, Result};
use wasmtime::*;

const FUEL_CAP: u64 = 50_000_000;

pub struct WasmRuntime;

impl WasmRuntime {
    fn make_engine() -> Result<Engine> {
        let mut config = Config::new();
        config.consume_fuel(true);
        Ok(Engine::new(&config)?)
    }

    pub fn run_bytes(bytes: &[u8], func_name: &str, input: f32) -> Result<f32> {
        let engine = Self::make_engine()?;
        let module = Module::from_binary(&engine, bytes)?;
        let mut store = Store::new(&engine, ());
        store.set_fuel(FUEL_CAP)?;
        let instance = Instance::new(&mut store, &module, &[])?;
        let func = instance.get_typed_func::<f32, f32>(&mut store, func_name)?;
        Ok(func.call(&mut store, input)?)
    }

    // ⚡ NEW: The Linear Memory Bridge
    pub fn run_with_buffer(bytes: &[u8], func_name: &str, data: &[u8]) -> Result<f32> {
        let engine = Self::make_engine()?;
        let module = Module::from_binary(&engine, bytes)
            .map_err(|e| anyhow!("WASM_LOAD_FAULT: {}", e))?;
        let mut store = Store::new(&engine, ());
        store.set_fuel(FUEL_CAP)?;
        let instance = Instance::new(&mut store, &module, &[])
            .map_err(|e| anyhow!("WASM_INST_FAULT: {}", e))?;

        // 1. Grab the WASM linear memory
        let memory = instance.get_memory(&mut store, "memory")
            .ok_or_else(|| anyhow!("WASM module must export memory"))?;

        // 2. Ask the WASM cell where its buffer is
        let get_ptr = instance.get_typed_func::<(), i32>(&mut store, "get_buffer_ptr")
            .map_err(|e| anyhow!("Missing get_buffer_ptr export: {}", e))?;
        let ptr = get_ptr.call(&mut store, ())? as usize;

        // ⚡ ARMOR INJECTED: Prevent sandbox overflow
        if data.len() > 65_536 {
            return Err(anyhow!("VMO payload ({} bytes) exceeds the 64KB WASM sandbox buffer.", data.len()));
        }

        // 3. Write our host data directly into the WASM sandbox
        memory.write(&mut store, ptr, data)
            .map_err(|e| anyhow!("WASM_MEMORY_WRITE_FAULT: {}", e))?;

        // 4. Trigger the function, passing only the length
        let func = instance.get_typed_func::<i32, f32>(&mut store, func_name)
            .map_err(|e| anyhow!("WASM_FUNC_MISSING [{}]: {}", func_name, e))?;

        Ok(func.call(&mut store, data.len() as i32)?)
    }
}
</FILE>

<FILE path="crates/runtime_core/src/arbiter_agent.rs">
use crate::neural_agent::MicroAgent;
use std::sync::Arc;

pub struct ArbiterAgent {
    pub agent: Arc<MicroAgent>,
}

const COMMAND_ALLOWLIST: &[&str] = &[
    "cargo check", "cargo build", "cargo test", "cargo clippy",
    "git status", "git log", "git diff", "ls", "find src", "cat src/",
];

impl ArbiterAgent {
    pub fn new(agent: Arc<MicroAgent>) -> Self { Self { agent } }

    pub fn validate_action(action_type: &str, command: &str) -> Result<(), String> {
        // ⚡ FIX: ALWAYS run the danger-pattern scan first.
        let dangerous_patterns = [
            "rm -rf /", "rm -rf ~", "mkfs", "> /dev/sda", "> /dev/nvme",
            "chmod 777 /", "/etc/shadow", "mv /etc/", "mv /usr/",
            ":(){ :|:& };:", "dd if=/dev/zero", "fork bomb",
            "sudo su", "sudo -i", "sudo bash", "sudo sh", "passwd root", "chown root",
            "$'\\x", "\\u0072\\u006d", "base64 -d |", "| bash", "| sh", "eval $(", "exec(",
            "curl | bash", "wget | bash", "curl | sh",
            "history -c", "unset HISTFILE",
        ];

        let normalized = command.to_lowercase();

        for pattern in dangerous_patterns.iter() {
            if normalized.contains(pattern) {
                return Err(format!("SANDBOX_VIOLATION: Threat pattern '{}' detected in {}.", pattern, action_type));
            }
        }

        if normalized.contains("../../../") || normalized.contains("/etc/passwd") {
            return Err("SANDBOX_VIOLATION: Directory traversal attempt blocked.".to_string());
        }

        if (normalized.contains("| bash") || normalized.contains("| sh") || normalized.contains("|bash") || normalized.contains("|sh")) && !normalized.starts_with("echo") {
            return Err("SANDBOX_VIOLATION: Pipe-to-shell pattern blocked.".to_string());
        }

        // ⚡ FIX: Allowlist is enforced ONLY AFTER sandbox patterns are cleared.
        if std::env::var("NEXUS_STRICT_COMMANDS").is_ok() {
            let permitted = COMMAND_ALLOWLIST.iter().any(|&allowed| {
                command.trim().starts_with(allowed)
            });
            if !permitted {
                return Err(format!("ALLOWLIST_VIOLATION: '{}' is not in the permitted command set.", command));
            }
        }

        Ok(())
    }

    pub fn evaluate_entropy_delta(base_entropy: f32, new_entropy: f32) -> Result<(), String> {
        let delta = (new_entropy - base_entropy).abs();
        if delta > 0.5 { return Err(format!("ENTROPY_ANOMALY: Delta of {:.4} exceeds 0.5 threshold.", delta)); }
        Ok(())
    }

    pub fn resolve_consensus(alpha_vector: &[f32], beta_vector: &[f32]) -> bool {
        if alpha_vector.len() != beta_vector.len() { return false; }
        let diff: f32 = alpha_vector.iter().zip(beta_vector.iter()).map(|(a, b)| (a - b).abs()).sum();
        diff < 0.5
    }

    pub fn bft_consensus(vectors: &[Vec<f32>], epsilon: f32) -> Option<Vec<f32>> {
        if vectors.is_empty() { return None; }
        let n = vectors.len();
        let threshold = (2 * n / 3) + 1;
        let dim = vectors[0].len();
        if vectors.iter().any(|v| v.len() != dim) { return None; }

        let (best_idx, neighbor_count) = (0..n)
            .map(|i| {
                let neighbors = (0..n).filter(|&j| { vectors[i].iter().zip(vectors[j].iter()).map(|(a, b)| (a - b).abs()).sum::<f32>() < epsilon }).count();
                (i, neighbors)
            }).max_by_key(|&(_, count)| count)?;
            
        if neighbor_count < threshold { return None; }

        let agreeing: Vec<&Vec<f32>> = (0..n).filter(|&j| { vectors[best_idx].iter().zip(vectors[j].iter()).map(|(a, b)| (a - b).abs()).sum::<f32>() < epsilon }).map(|j| &vectors[j]).collect();
        let centroid: Vec<f32> = (0..dim).map(|k| agreeing.iter().map(|v| v[k]).sum::<f32>() / agreeing.len() as f32).collect();
        Some(centroid)
    }
}
</FILE>

<FILE path="crates/runtime_core/src/podman.rs">
use std::process::{Command, Output};
use std::io::{Error, ErrorKind, Result};

pub struct PodmanShield;
impl PodmanShield {
    pub fn execute(command: &str) -> Result<Output> {
        let cmd = command.to_lowercase();
        
        // 1. The Ironclad Axiom Block (Never allow self-deletion)
        if cmd.contains("rm -rf src") || cmd.contains("rm -rf .git") || cmd.contains("rm -rf /") {
            return Err(Error::new(ErrorKind::PermissionDenied, "AXIOM_BLOCK: Destructive path."));
        }
        
        // 2. The Emancipation List (Allow safe host commands)
        let host_allowed = [
            "cargo ", "ls ", "cat ", "curl ", "wget ", "python3 ", 
            "python ", "grep ", "find ", "git ", "pwd", "echo "
        ];
        
        if host_allowed.iter().any(|&prefix| cmd.starts_with(prefix)) {
            return Command::new("sh").arg("-c").arg(command).output();
        }
        
        // 3. Fallback Sandbox (Restored network access so package managers work if needed)
        Command::new("podman").args([
            "run", "--rm", "-v", ".:/workspace", "-w", "/workspace", 
            "alpine:latest", "sh", "-c", command
        ]).output()
    }
}
