<FILE path="crates/runtime_core/src/diff.rs">
/**
 * NEXUS Diff Engine — v2.0 [SURGICAL + FUZZY]
 * Copyright 2026 Donald Ball
 * Handles precise edits even when LLM mangles whitespace or line order.
 */

use anyhow::{anyhow, Result};

pub struct DiffEngine;

impl DiffEngine {
    /// Primary entry point: tries exact match first, then fuzzy/indentation-agnostic.
    pub fn apply(original: &str, search: &str, replace: &str) -> Result<String> {
        if search.trim().is_empty() {
            return Ok(original.to_string());
        }

        // Tier 1: Exact match (fastest, most precise)
        if let Some(pos) = original.find(search) {
            return Ok(replace_first_occurrence(original, pos, search.len(), replace));
        }

        // Tier 2: Indentation-agnostic matching (handles LLM whitespace issues)
        if let Some(result) = Self::apply_agnostic(original, search, replace) {
            return Ok(result);
        }

        // Tier 3: Fuzzy line-based matching (for larger blocks)
        if let Some(result) = Self::apply_fuzzy_lines(original, search, replace) {
            return Ok(result);
        }

        Err(anyhow!(
            "PATTERN_NOT_FOUND: Could not locate search block.\n\
            Search preview (first 120 chars):\n{}\n\n\
            Hint: Try making the search string more unique or shorter.",
            search.chars().take(120).collect::<String>()
        ))
    }

    fn apply_agnostic(original: &str, search: &str, replace: &str) -> Option<String> {
        let search_lines: Vec<&str> = search.lines().map(|l| l.trim_start()).collect();
        let orig_lines: Vec<&str> = original.lines().collect();
        let search_len = search_lines.len();

        if search_len == 0 || orig_lines.len() < search_len {
            return None;
        }

        for i in 0..=(orig_lines.len() - search_len) {
            let window = &orig_lines[i..i + search_len];
            let window_trimmed: Vec<&str> = window.iter().map(|l| l.trim_start()).collect();

            if window_trimmed == search_lines {
                let mut result = orig_lines[..i].join("\n");
                if !result.is_empty() { result.push('\n'); }
                result.push_str(replace);
                if i + search_len < orig_lines.len() {
                    result.push('\n');
                    result.push_str(&orig_lines[i + search_len..].join("\n"));
                }
                return Some(result);
            }
        }
        None
    }

    fn apply_fuzzy_lines(original: &str, search: &str, replace: &str) -> Option<String> {
        // Very coarse fallback: if search is long, try to match by first and last line
        let search_lines: Vec<&str> = search.lines().collect();
        if search_lines.len() < 3 {
            return None;
        }

        let first = search_lines[0].trim();
        let last = search_lines.last().unwrap().trim();

        let orig_lines: Vec<&str> = original.lines().collect();

        for i in 0..orig_lines.len() {
            if orig_lines[i].trim() == first {
                for j in (i + 2)..orig_lines.len() {
                    if orig_lines[j].trim() == last {
                        let mut result = orig_lines[..i].join("\n");
                        if !result.is_empty() { result.push('\n'); }
                        result.push_str(replace);
                        if j + 1 < orig_lines.len() {
                            result.push('\n');
                            result.push_str(&orig_lines[j + 1..].join("\n"));
                        }
                        return Some(result);
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        None
    }

    pub fn unified_diff(_old: &str, _new: &str, path: &str) -> String {
        format!("--- a/{}\n+++ b/{}\n[MODIFICATION APPLIED]", path, path)
    }
}

fn replace_first_occurrence(s: &str, pos: usize, old_len: usize, new: &str) -> String {
    let mut result = String::with_capacity(s.len() - old_len + new.len());
    result.push_str(&s[..pos]);
    result.push_str(new);
    result.push_str(&s[pos + old_len..]);
    result
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn test_exact_match() {
        let src = "fn old() {}\nfn keep() {}";
        let result = DiffEngine::apply(src, "fn old() {}", "fn new() {}").unwrap();
        assert_eq!(result, "fn new() {}\nfn keep() {}");
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_agnostic_whitespace() {
        let src = "    fn foo() {\n        println!(\"hi\");\n    }";
        let search = "fn foo() {\n    println!(\"hi\");\n}";
        let replace = "fn bar() {\n    println!(\"hello\");\n}";
        let result = DiffEngine::apply(src, search, replace).unwrap();
        assert!(result.contains("fn bar()"));
    }
}
</FILE>

<FILE path="crates/runtime_core/src/ast_editor.rs">
use tree_sitter::{Parser, Query, QueryCursor, StreamingIterator};
use std::fs;
use anyhow::{anyhow, Result};

pub struct AstEditor;

impl AstEditor {
    /// update_function: Finds a function by name and replaces its entire body.
    /// This is the "Semantic Precision" upgrade to replace simple string diffs.
    pub fn update_function(path: &str, function_name: &str, new_body: &str) -> Result<String> {
        let source_code = fs::read_to_string(path)?;
        let mut parser = Parser::new();
        parser.set_language(&tree_sitter_rust::LANGUAGE.into())?;
        
        let tree = parser.parse(&source_code, None)
            .ok_or_else(|| anyhow!("AST_PARSE_FAIL: Could not parse {}", path))?;
            
        // Query to find the specific function node
        let query_str = format!(
            "(function_item name: (identifier) @name (#eq? @name \"{}\")) @func",
            function_name
        );
        let query = Query::new(&tree_sitter_rust::LANGUAGE.into(), &query_str)?;
        let mut cursor = QueryCursor::new();
        let mut matches = cursor.matches(&query, tree.root_node(), source_code.as_bytes());
        while let Some(m) = matches.next() {
            for capture in m.captures {
                if capture.index == 1 { // The @func capture
                    let node = capture.node;
                    let start = node.start_byte();
                    let end = node.end_byte();
                    
                    let mut new_code = source_code.clone();
                    new_code.replace_range(start..end, new_body);
                    return Ok(new_code);
                }
            }
        }
        
        Err(anyhow!("FUNCTION_NOT_FOUND: Could not find function '{}' in {}", function_name, path))
    }
}
</FILE>

<FILE path="crates/runtime_core/src/cluster.rs">
// cluster.rs — v1.1
// FIX: duration_since().unwrap() → unwrap_or_default() to survive NTP jumps.
use parking_lot::RwLock;
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use std::sync::Arc;
use std::time::{SystemTime, UNIX_EPOCH};

#[derive(Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, Debug)]
pub struct ClusterNode {
    pub id: String,
    pub host: String,
    pub port: u16,
    pub cpu: f32,
    pub ram: f32,
    pub temp: f32,
    pub last_seen: u64,
}

#[derive(Clone)]
pub struct ClusterMesh {
    nodes: Arc<RwLock<Vec<ClusterNode>>>,
}

fn now_secs() -> u64 {
    SystemTime::now()
        .duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH)
        .unwrap_or_default()
        .as_secs()
}

impl ClusterMesh {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self { nodes: Arc::new(RwLock::new(vec![])) }
    }

    pub fn register(&self, mut node: ClusterNode) {
        node.last_seen = now_secs();
        let mut guard = self.nodes.write();
        if let Some(existing) = guard.iter_mut().find(|n| n.id == node.id) {
            *existing = node;
        } else {
            guard.push(node);
        }
    }

    pub fn get_all_nodes(&self) -> Vec<ClusterNode> { self.nodes.read().clone() }

    pub fn best_node(&self) -> Option<ClusterNode> {
        let now = now_secs();
        self.nodes
            .read()
            .iter()
            .filter(|n| now.saturating_sub(n.last_seen) < 40)
            .cloned()
            .min_by(|a, b| {
                (a.cpu + a.ram)
                    .partial_cmp(&(b.cpu + b.ram))
                    .unwrap_or(std::cmp::Ordering::Equal)
            })
    }
}
</FILE>

<FILE path="crates/runtime_core/src/distributed_scheduler.rs">
// src/distributed_scheduler.rs — v2.0 [PRE-EMPTIVE FORGE OFFLOAD]
use anyhow::{anyhow, Result};
use base64::{engine::general_purpose, Engine as _};
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use std::sync::Arc;
use crate::cluster::ClusterMesh;
use crate::wasm_runtime::WasmRuntime;

#[derive(Clone)]
pub struct DistributedScheduler {
    pub mesh: Arc<ClusterMesh>,
}

impl DistributedScheduler {
    pub fn new(mesh: Arc<ClusterMesh>) -> Self {
        Self { mesh }
    }

    pub async fn synthesize(&self, code: &str) -> Result<String> {
        if let Some(node) = self.mesh.best_node() {
            if node.cpu < 85.0 && node.temp < 68.0 {
                 println!(
                    "\x1b[35m>> [MESH_ROUTING] Offloading WASM Synthesis to {} (Temp: {:.1}°C Load: {:.1}%)\x1b[0m",
                    node.id, node.temp, node.cpu
                );
                return self.remote_synthesize(&node.host, node.port, code).await;
            }
        }
        
        println!("\x1b[33m>> [LOCAL_EXEC] Substrate utilized for WASM synthesis (Heavy Load)\x1b[0m");
        crate::wasm_forge::WasmForge::synthesize_cell(code).await
    }

    async fn remote_synthesize(&self, host: &str, port: u16, code: &str) -> Result<String> {
        #[derive(Serialize)]
        struct Req<'a> { code: &'a str }
        #[derive(Deserialize)]
        struct Reply { ok: bool, b64: Option<String>, error: Option<String> }

        let url = format!("http://{}:{}/api/cluster/forge", host, port);
        let res = reqwest::Client::new()
            .post(&url)
            .header("Authorization", "SOVEREIGN")
            .json(&Req { code })
            .send()
            .await?;

        let reply: Reply = res.json().await?;
        if reply.ok { 
            Ok(reply.b64.unwrap_or_default()) 
        } else { 
            Err(anyhow!(reply.error.unwrap_or_else(|| "Remote forge error".into()))) 
        }
    }

    pub async fn execute(&self, wasm: Vec<u8>, func_name: &str, input: f32) -> Result<f32> {
        if let Some(node) = self.mesh.best_node() {
            if node.cpu < 85.0 && node.temp < 68.0 {
                 println!(
                    ">> [MESH_ROUTING] Offloading execution of {} to {}",
                    func_name, node.id
                );
                return self.remote_call(&node.host, node.port, wasm, func_name, input).await;
            }
        }
        
        println!(">> [LOCAL_EXEC] Substrate utilized for {}", func_name);
        WasmRuntime::run_bytes(&wasm, func_name, input)
    }

    async fn remote_call(&self, host: &str, port: u16, wasm: Vec<u8>, func_name: &str, input: f32) -> Result<f32> {
        #[derive(Serialize)]
        struct Req<'a> { wasm_b64: String, func_name: &'a str, input: f32 }
        #[derive(Deserialize)]
        struct Reply { ok: bool, result: f32, error: Option<String> }

        let url = format!("http://{}:{}/api/cluster/run", host, port);
        let res = reqwest::Client::new()
            .post(&url)
            .header("Authorization", "SOVEREIGN")
            .json(&Req { wasm_b64: general_purpose::STANDARD.encode(&wasm), func_name, input })
            .send()
            .await?;

        let reply: Reply = res.json().await?;
        if reply.ok { Ok(reply.result) } 
        else { Err(anyhow!(reply.error.unwrap_or_else(|| "remote error".into()))) }
    }
}
</FILE>

pub async fn inject_workspace_dna(directive: &str) -> String {
    directive.trim().to_string()
}

pub async fn get_execution_law_v6() -> String {
    r#"You are NEXUS — a silent, precise, rule-bound autonomous coding agent.

=== ABSOLUTE OUTPUT RULES (VIOLATION = SYSTEM FAILURE) ===
- Output **ONLY** a valid JSON array of AgentAction objects.
- NO explanations, NO thoughts, NO markdown, NO "I think", NO preamble.
- Response must start with `[` and end with `]`.
- Keep "reasoning" fields very short (max 12 words).

=== ONE LOGICAL STEP PER TURN ===
- Do exactly one meaningful action per response.
- After read_file / diff_patch / write_file / execute_command, stop and wait for next turn.
- If mission is complete based on observations, output only `halt`.

=== EDITING PREFERENCE ===
- Prefer `diff_patch` for small surgical changes.
- Use `patch_function` only when asked for semantic/AST-level function replacement.
- Use `read_file` first when you need to see current code.

=== READ_FILE PROTOCOL ===
- When you see [READ_OK] with --- CONTENT --- block, use that content directly.
- Never re-read the same file if content is already provided.

Valid actions only:
- "read_file"      { "path": "...", "reasoning": "..." }
- "write_file"     { "path": "...", "content": "...", "reasoning": "..." }
- "diff_patch"     { "path": "...", "search": "...", "replace": "...", "reasoning": "..." }
- "patch_function" { "path": "...", "function_name": "...", "new_body": "...", "reasoning": "..." }
- "execute_command"{ "command": "...", "reasoning": "..." }
- "halt"           { "reasoning": "..." }

Now execute the directive. Output ONLY the JSON array."#.to_string()
}
</FILE>

<FILE path="crates/runtime_core/src/executor.rs">
// src/executor.rs — v2.7 [IRONCLAD_FSM + AGENTIC_LRL]

use crate::agent_protocol::AgentAction;
use crate::arbiter_agent::ArbiterAgent;
use crate::distributed_scheduler::DistributedScheduler;
use crate::shared_state::VmoState;
use anyhow::Result;
use std::sync::Arc;
use tokio::sync::Mutex;

#[derive(Debug, PartialEq, Clone)]
pub enum FsmState { Idle, Executing, Complete, Fault }

pub struct RoboticExecutor {
    pub current_state: FsmState,
    tx: tokio::sync::broadcast::Sender<String>,
}

impl RoboticExecutor {
    pub fn new(_api_key: String, tx: tokio::sync::broadcast::Sender<String>) -> Self {
        Self { current_state: FsmState::Idle, tx }
    }

    pub async fn execute_handle(
        &mut self,
        actions: Vec<AgentAction>,
        memory: Arc<Mutex<nexus_substrate::memory::MemoryPalace>>,
        vmo_state: Arc<Mutex<VmoState>>,
        dist: Arc<DistributedScheduler>,
    ) -> Result<Vec<String>> {
        self.current_state = FsmState::Executing;
        let mut observations = Vec::new();
        let total = actions.len();

        for (idx, action) in actions.into_iter().enumerate() {
            let _ = self.tx.send(serde_json::json!({
                "type": "TASK_STATUS",
                "payload": format!("Executing → {:?}", action)
            }).to_string());

            if matches!(action, AgentAction::Halt { .. }) {
            }

            match self.execute_single_action(action, memory.clone(), vmo_state.clone(), dist.clone()).await {
                Ok(obs) => {
                    observations.push(obs.clone());
                    tracing::info!("Action observation: {}", obs);

                    if obs.contains("[READ_OK]") || obs.contains("[WRITE_OK]") || obs.contains("[CMD:") || obs.contains("[DIFF_PATCH_OK]") || obs.contains("[PATCH_OK]") || obs.contains("[REPORT_OK]") || obs.contains("[HALT]") {
                        let skipped = total.saturating_sub(idx + 1);
                        if skipped > 0 {
                            let yield_msg = format!(">> [YIELD] Action completed. {} action(s) suspended for next turn.", skipped);
                            observations.push(yield_msg.clone());
                            tracing::info!("{}", yield_msg);
                        }
                        break;
                    }
                }
                Err(e) => {
                    let err_obs = format!("ACTION_FAILED: {}", e);
                    observations.push(err_obs.clone());
                    tracing::warn!("Action failed: {}", e);
                    self.current_state = FsmState::Fault;
                    return Ok(observations);
                }
            }
        }

        self.current_state = FsmState::Complete;
        Ok(observations)
    }

    async fn execute_single_action(
        &mut self,
        action: AgentAction,
        _memory: Arc<Mutex<nexus_substrate::memory::MemoryPalace>>,
        _vmo_state: Arc<Mutex<VmoState>>,
        _dist: Arc<DistributedScheduler>,
    ) -> Result<String> {
        match action {
            AgentAction::ExecuteCommand { command, reasoning } => {
                let _ = self.tx.send(serde_json::json!({"type": "NEURAL_THOUGHT", "payload": format!("Reasoning: {}", reasoning)}).to_string());
                if let Err(e) = ArbiterAgent::validate_action("execute_command", &command) {
                    return Ok(format!("SHIELD_BLOCKED: {}", e));
                }
                
                // ⚡ LRL FUSION: Route strictly through AgenticRuntime
                let mut lrl = crate::agents::agentic_runtime::AgenticRuntime::new(
                    crate::agents::agentic_runtime::SubstrateContext {
                        memory: _memory.clone(),
                        vmo_state: _vmo_state.clone(),
                        dist: _dist.clone(),
                    }
                );
                
                // Inject the ecosystem
                lrl.register_agent(Box::new(crate::agents::scout_agent::ScoutAgent::new()));
                lrl.register_agent(Box::new(crate::agents::shell_agent::ShellAgent));
                
                match lrl.dispatch(&command).await {
                    Ok(output) => Ok(format!("[CMD:LRL_ROUTED]\n{}\nReason: {}", output, reasoning)),
                    Err(e) => Ok(format!("LRL_FAULT: {}", e)),
                }
            }

            AgentAction::WriteFile { path, content, reasoning } => {
                let _ = self.tx.send(serde_json::json!({"type": "NEURAL_THOUGHT", "payload": format!("Writing file: {} | Reason: {}", path, reasoning)}).to_string());
                let content_len = content.len();
                let preview: String = content.chars().take(80).collect();
                tokio::fs::write(&path, content).await
                    .map(|_| format!("[WRITE_OK] {} bytes → {} | Preview: {}…", content_len, path, preview))
                    .map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!("WRITE_FAILED: {}", e))
            }

            AgentAction::ReadFile { path, reasoning } => {
                let _ = self.tx.send(serde_json::json!({"type": "NEURAL_THOUGHT", "payload": format!("Reading file: {} | Reason: {}", path, reasoning)}).to_string());
                match tokio::fs::read_to_string(&path).await {
                    Ok(content) => {
                        let byte_len = content.len();
                        const MAX_CHARS: usize = 4096;
                        let char_count = content.chars().count();
                        let injected: String = content.chars().take(MAX_CHARS).collect();
                        let note = if char_count > MAX_CHARS {
                            format!("\n[TRUNCATED: showing {}/{} chars — use diff_patch for large files]", MAX_CHARS, char_count)
                        } else { String::new() };
                        Ok(format!("[READ_OK] {} ({} bytes){}\n--- CONTENT ---\n{}\n--- END CONTENT ---", path, byte_len, note, injected))
                    }
                    Err(e) => anyhow::bail!("READ_FAILED: {}", e),
                }
            }

            AgentAction::DiffPatch { path, search, replace, reasoning } => {
                let _ = self.tx.send(serde_json::json!({"type": "NEURAL_THOUGHT", "payload": format!("Applying diff patch to {} | Reason: {}", path, reasoning)}).to_string());
                match tokio::fs::read_to_string(&path).await {
                    Ok(original) => {
                        let orig_lines = original.lines().count();
                        match crate::diff::DiffEngine::apply(&original, &search, &replace) {
                            Ok(new_content) => {
                                let new_lines: usize = new_content.lines().count();
                                match tokio::fs::write(&path, &new_content).await {
                                    Ok(_) => Ok(format!("[DIFF_PATCH_OK] {} | Lines: {} → {}", path, orig_lines, new_lines)),
                                    Err(e) => Ok(format!("[DIFF_PATCH_WRITE_FAILED] {}", e)),
                                }
                            }
                            Err(e) => Ok(format!("[DIFF_PATCH_FAILED] {}", e)),
                        }
                    }
                    Err(e) => Ok(format!("[DIFF_PATCH_READ_FAILED] {}", e)),
                }
            }

            AgentAction::PatchFunction { path, function_name, new_body, reasoning } => {
                let _ = self.tx.send(serde_json::json!({"type": "NEURAL_THOUGHT", "payload": format!("Patching function {} in {} | Reason: {}", function_name, path, reasoning)}).to_string());
                match crate::ast_editor::AstEditor::update_function(&path, &function_name, &new_body) {
                    Ok(full_code) => {
                        match tokio::fs::write(&path, full_code).await {
                            Ok(_) => Ok(format!("[PATCH_OK] Function '{}' updated in {}", function_name, path)),
                            Err(e) => Ok(format!("PATCH_WRITE_FAILED: {}", e)),
                        }
                    },
                    Err(e) => Ok(format!("PATCH_FAILED: {}", e)),
                }
            }

            AgentAction::ExportReport { title, content, reasoning } => {
                let _ = self.tx.send(serde_json::json!({"type": "NEURAL_THOUGHT", "payload": format!("Exporting report: {} | Reason: {}", title, reasoning)}).to_string());
                let safe_name = title.to_lowercase().replace(|c: char| !c.is_alphanumeric() && c != ' ' && c != '-', "_");
                let path = format!("src/{}.md", safe_name);
                tokio::fs::write(&path, content).await
                    .map(|_| format!("[REPORT_OK] Exported → {}", path))
                    .map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!("EXPORT_FAILED: {}", e))
            }

            AgentAction::FetchUiSnapshot { reasoning } => {
                let _ = self.tx.send(serde_json::json!({"type": "LOG_EVENT", "payload": format!("fetch_ui_snapshot triggered — {}", reasoning)}).to_string());
                Ok("[UI_SNAPSHOT_REQUESTED] Ghost DOM state capture requested.".to_string())
            }

            AgentAction::Halt { reasoning } => {
                self.current_state = FsmState::Complete;
                let clean_reason = reasoning.chars().take(100).collect::<String>();
                let _ = self.tx.send(serde_json::json!({"type": "TASK_STATUS", "payload": format!("HALT: {}", clean_reason)}).to_string());
                Ok(format!("[HALT] {}", clean_reason))
            }

            _ => Ok(format!("[STUB_OK] Action acknowledged: {:?}", action))
        }
    }
}
</FILE>

<FILE path="crates/runtime_core/src/task_manager.rs">
use nexus_substrate::memory::MemoryPalace;
use crate::{
    agent_protocol::AgentAction, cli_utils, json_parser, logic_engine::LogicEngine,
    metrics::METRICS, orchestrator::Orchestrator, executor::RoboticExecutor,
    shared_state::VmoState, distributed_scheduler::DistributedScheduler,
};
use reqwest::Client;
use std::sync::Arc;
use tokio::sync::{broadcast, Mutex};
use tracing::info;

pub async fn send_task(
    orchestrator: Arc<Orchestrator>,
    client: &Client,
    api_key: &str,
    raw_directive: &str,
    memory: Arc<Mutex<MemoryPalace>>,
    vmo_state: Arc<Mutex<VmoState>>,
    tx: broadcast::Sender<String>,
    dist: Arc<DistributedScheduler>,
) -> String {
    METRICS.inc_task();
    let mut session_obs: Vec<String> = vec![];
    let mut history: Vec<(String, String)> = vec![]; 
    let mut turn = 0usize;
    let mut consecutive_fractures = 0usize;

    let execution_law = cli_utils::get_execution_law_v6().await;
    let mut executor = RoboticExecutor::new(api_key.to_string(), tx.clone());

    'mission: while turn < 12 {
        turn += 1;
        info!("=== Turn {} / 12 ===", turn);

        if LogicEngine::detect_deadlock(&session_obs) { break 'mission; }
        if consecutive_fractures >= 3 { break 'mission; }

        let mut full_prompt = String::new();
        for (q, a) in &history { full_prompt.push_str(&format!("USER: {}\nMODEL: {}\n", q, a)); }
        
        let turn_prompt = if turn == 1 { raw_directive.to_string() } 
                          else { format!("The last action resulted in: {}. What is the next logical step?", 
                                         session_obs.last().cloned().unwrap_or_else(|| "SUCCESS".into())) };
        full_prompt.push_str(&format!("USER: {}", turn_prompt));

        let ctx = crate::neural_agent::ContextPacket {
            system_prompt: Some(execution_law.clone()),
            recent_logs: session_obs.iter().rev().take(3).rev().cloned().collect(),
            ..Default::default()
        };

        let response = match orchestrator.run(full_prompt, ctx, client, api_key, Some(tx.clone())).await {
            Ok(resp) => resp,
            Err(e) => { let _ = tx.send(serde_json::json!({"type": "TASK_STATUS", "payload": format!("UPLINK FAILED: {}", e)}).to_string()); tracing::error!("Uplink failed: {}", e); break 'mission; }
        };

        match json_parser::extract_actions(&response) {
            Ok(actions) => {
                if actions.is_empty() { break 'mission; }
                history.push((turn_prompt, response.clone()));
                consecutive_fractures = 0;
                match executor.execute_handle(actions.clone(), memory.clone(), vmo_state.clone(), dist.clone()).await {
                    Ok(observations) => { for obs in observations { session_obs.push(obs); } }
                    Err(e) => session_obs.push(format!("EXECUTOR_FAULT: {}", e)),
                }
                if actions.iter().any(|a| matches!(a, AgentAction::Halt { .. })) { break 'mission; }
            }
            Err(_) => {
                consecutive_fractures += 1;
                session_obs.push("[FORMAT_VIOLATION] Output ONLY a raw JSON array.".into());
            }
        }
    }
    "TASK_COMPLETE".to_string()
}
</FILE>

<FILE path="crates/runtime_core/src/lazarus_vault.rs">
// lazarus_vault.rs — v1.4 [LEDGER_FIX]
// FIX: auto_snapshot() now appends every snapshot to ledger.json.
//      Previously it wrote to .nexus/vault/<hash> but never touched the ledger,
//      so list_snapshots() returned [] and rollback was permanently blind.
// FIX: list_snapshots() returns entries sorted oldest→newest so
//      .last() in the rollback path gives the most recent stable state.
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use std::collections::hash_map::DefaultHasher;
use std::fs;
use std::hash::{Hash, Hasher};
use std::path::Path;
use std::time::SystemTime;

#[derive(Debug, Serialize, Deserialize, Clone)]
pub struct Snapshot {
    pub id:        String,
    pub path:      String,
    pub hash:      String,
    pub timestamp: String,
    pub directive: String,
}

pub struct LazarusVault;

impl LazarusVault {
    const VAULT_DIR:   &'static str = ".nexus/vault";
    const LEDGER_PATH: &'static str = ".nexus/vault/ledger.json";

    /// Snapshot a single file to the vault and append to ledger.
    pub async fn auto_snapshot(path: &str, tag: &str) -> Result<Snapshot, String> {
        // Reject directories — vault only snapshots files
        let metadata = fs::metadata(path)
            .map_err(|e| format!("PATH_NOT_FOUND: {}", e))?;
        if metadata.is_dir() {
            return Err("DIRECTORY_SNAPSHOT_NOT_SUPPORTED: Specify a file.".into());
        }

        let content = fs::read_to_string(path)
            .map_err(|e| format!("FILE_READ_FAULT: {}", e))?;

        // Content hash for deduplication
        let mut hasher = DefaultHasher::new();
        content.hash(&mut hasher);
        let hash = format!("{:x}", hasher.finish());

        fs::create_dir_all(Self::VAULT_DIR)
            .map_err(|e| format!("DIR_FAULT: {}", e))?;

        let dest = format!("{}/{}", Self::VAULT_DIR, hash);
        fs::write(&dest, &content)
            .map_err(|e| format!("FS_WRITE_FAULT: {}", e))?;

        let snapshot = Snapshot {
            id:        hash.clone(),
            path:      path.to_string(),
            hash:      hash.clone(),
            timestamp: format!("{:?}", SystemTime::now()),
            directive: tag.to_string(),
        };

        // FIX: Actually persist to ledger so list_snapshots() can find it
        Self::append_to_ledger(&snapshot)?;

        println!(
            "\x1b[32m[LAZARUS] Snapshot {} → {} [tag: {}]\x1b[0m",
            &hash[..8.min(hash.len())], path, tag
        );

        Ok(snapshot)
    }

    /// Restore a snapshot by hash — writes the saved content back to its path.
    pub fn restore(hash: &str) -> Result<String, String> {
        let src = format!("{}/{}", Self::VAULT_DIR, hash);
        let content = fs::read_to_string(&src)
            .map_err(|e| format!("VAULT_READ_FAULT [{}]: {}", hash, e))?;

        // Find the target path from the ledger
        let snap = Self::list_snapshots()
            .into_iter()
            .find(|s| s.hash == hash)
            .ok_or_else(|| format!("HASH_NOT_IN_LEDGER: {}", hash))?;

        fs::write(&snap.path, &content)
            .map_err(|e| format!("RESTORE_WRITE_FAULT: {}", e))?;

        Ok(format!("[LAZARUS] Restored {} → {}", &hash[..8.min(hash.len())], snap.path))
    }

    /// Appends a snapshot to ledger.json. Deduplicates by hash.
    fn append_to_ledger(snapshot: &Snapshot) -> Result<(), String> {
        let mut entries = Self::list_snapshots();

        if entries.iter().any(|e| e.hash == snapshot.hash) {
            return Ok(()); // Already snapshotted identical content
        }

        entries.push(snapshot.clone());

        let json = serde_json::to_string_pretty(&entries)
            .map_err(|e| format!("LEDGER_SERIALIZE_FAULT: {}", e))?;

        fs::write(Self::LEDGER_PATH, json)
            .map_err(|e| format!("LEDGER_WRITE_FAULT: {}", e))?;

        Ok(())
    }

    /// Returns all snapshots sorted oldest → newest.
    /// Use .last() for the most recent stable snapshot.
    pub fn list_snapshots() -> Vec<Snapshot> {
        if !Path::new(Self::LEDGER_PATH).exists() {
            return vec![];
        }
        let data = fs::read_to_string(Self::LEDGER_PATH).unwrap_or_default();
        let mut entries: Vec<Snapshot> = serde_json::from_str(&data).unwrap_or_default();
        entries.sort_by(|a, b| a.timestamp.cmp(&b.timestamp));
        entries
    }

    /// Returns the most recent snapshot for a given file path.
    pub fn latest_for_path(path: &str) -> Option<Snapshot> {
        Self::list_snapshots()
            .into_iter()
            .filter(|s| s.path == path)
            .last()
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use std::fs;

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn test_snapshot_and_ledger() {
        let test_file = "/tmp/nexus_test_snapshot.rs";
        fs::write(test_file, "fn main() {}").unwrap();

        let snap = LazarusVault::auto_snapshot(test_file, "test_tag")
            .await
            .expect("snapshot failed");

        assert!(!snap.hash.is_empty());
        assert_eq!(snap.path, test_file);

        let ledger = LazarusVault::list_snapshots();
        assert!(ledger.iter().any(|e| e.hash == snap.hash));

        fs::remove_file(test_file).ok();
    }
}
</FILE>

<FILE path="crates/runtime_core/src/inquisitor.rs">
// inquisitor.rs — v1.1 [UI_AUDIT_RESTORED]
// Autonomous workspace auditor. Called by SelfHealingEngine on each cycle.
use std::fs;
use std::path::Path;
use std::time::SystemTime;
use tokio::process::Command;

pub struct Inquisitor;

impl Inquisitor {
    /// audit_ui: Checks for missing critical Svelte/UI files.
    pub async fn audit_ui() -> anyhow::Result<String> {
        let critical_paths = ["ui/src/App.svelte", "ui/package.json", "ui/index.html"];

        for path in critical_paths {
            if !Path::new(path).exists() {
                return Ok(format!("FRACTURE: Missing critical UI file: {}", path));
            }
        }

        if !Path::new("ui/node_modules").exists() {
            return Ok("FRACTURE: UI dependencies (node_modules) missing. Run npm install.".into());
        }

        Ok("NOMINAL".into())
    }

    /// audit_backend: Runs `cargo check` and returns errors or "NOMINAL".
    pub async fn audit_backend() -> anyhow::Result<String> {
        let output = Command::new("cargo")
            .args(["check"])
            .output()
            .await
            .map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!("Cargo check failed: {}", e))?;

        if output.status.success() {
            Ok("NOMINAL".into())
        } else {
            Ok(String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stderr).to_string())
        }
    }

    /// diagnose_workspace: Runs both audits and returns a combined report.
    pub async fn diagnose_workspace() -> anyhow::Result<String> {
        let backend = Self::audit_backend().await.unwrap_or_else(|e| e.to_string());
        let ui      = Self::audit_ui().await.unwrap_or_else(|e| e.to_string());

        if backend == "NOMINAL" && ui == "NOMINAL" {
            return Ok("WORKSPACE_NOMINAL: Integrity verified across Rust and Svelte domains.".into());
        }

        let mut diagnostic = String::from("WORKSPACE_FRACTURE_DETECTED:\n");
        if backend != "NOMINAL" {
            diagnostic.push_str(&format!("--- BACKEND ERRORS ---\n{}\n", backend));
        }
        if ui != "NOMINAL" {
            diagnostic.push_str(&format!("--- FRONTEND ERRORS ---\n{}\n", ui));
        }

        Ok(diagnostic)
    }

    /// probe_shadow_activity: Detects files modified in the last 60 seconds.
    pub fn probe_shadow_activity() -> Vec<String> {
        let mut alerts = Vec::new();
        let targets = ["./src", "./ui/src"];

        for path in targets {
            if let Ok(meta) = fs::metadata(path) {
                if let Ok(modified) = meta.modified() {
                    if let Ok(elapsed) = SystemTime::now().duration_since(modified) {
                        if elapsed.as_secs() < 60 {
                            alerts.push(format!(
                                "SHADOW_ACTIVITY: {} modified recently ({}s ago)",
                                path, elapsed.as_secs()
                            ));
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        alerts
    }
}
</FILE>

<FILE path="crates/runtime_core/src/self_healing_engine.rs">
// self_healing_engine.rs — v4.1.2 [AUTONOMIC_STATE_MACHINE]
// Full FSM: Observing → Analyzing → Suturing → Verifying → RollingBack → Quarantine
// Runs as a background tokio task via ignite_autonomic_loop().
// Requires: Inquisitor (cargo check), LazarusVault (snapshots), GovernanceLog (audit trail)
use crate::{
    governance::protocol::GovernanceLog,
    inquisitor::Inquisitor,
    lazarus_vault::LazarusVault,
    metrics::METRICS,
    neural_agent::ContextPacket,
    agent_protocol::AgentAction,
};
use nexus_substrate::memory::MemoryPalace;
use parking_lot::Mutex;
use reqwest::Client;
use std::sync::{Arc, LazyLock};
use tokio::sync::Semaphore;
use tokio::time::{sleep, Duration};
use tracing::{error, info, warn};

/// Only one cargo check runs at a time — prevents runaway compile processes on
/// the 4GB Duet where concurrent compiles thrash RAM and eMMC swap.
static COMPILE_LOCK: LazyLock<Arc<Semaphore>> =
    LazyLock::new(|| Arc::new(Semaphore::new(1)));

/// Files snapshotted before every suture attempt so rollback is always possible.
const SNAPSHOT_TARGETS: &[&str] = &[
    "crates/runtime_core/src/executor.rs",
    "crates/runtime_core/src/task_manager.rs",
    "bins/ide_backend/src/server.rs",
    "bins/ide_backend/src/main.rs",
];

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub enum HealingState {
    Observing,
    Analyzing(String),
    Suturing(String),
    Verifying,
    RollingBack(String),
    Quarantine,
}

/// Shared context the engine needs to call the API and manage tasks.
/// Mirrors the fields from AppState that the engine actually uses.
pub struct HealingContext {
    pub client:          Client,
    pub api_key:         String,
    pub orchestrator:    Arc<crate::orchestrator::Orchestrator>,
    pub memory:          Arc<tokio::sync::Mutex<MemoryPalace>>,
    pub vmo_state:       Arc<tokio::sync::Mutex<crate::shared_state::VmoState>>,
    pub tx:              tokio::sync::broadcast::Sender<String>,
    pub dist:            Arc<crate::distributed_scheduler::DistributedScheduler>,
    pub governance_log:  Arc<Mutex<GovernanceLog>>,
    pub agent:           Arc<crate::neural_agent::MicroAgent>,
}

pub struct SelfHealingEngine {
    ctx:           Arc<HealingContext>,
    current_state: HealingState,
    failure_depth: u8,
}

impl SelfHealingEngine {
    pub fn new(ctx: Arc<HealingContext>) -> Self {
        Self {
            ctx,
            current_state: HealingState::Observing,
            failure_depth: 0,
        }
    }

    fn transition(&mut self, next: HealingState) {
        info!(
            "\x1b[35m[AIS_TRANSITION] {:?} → {:?}\x1b[0m",
            self.current_state, next
        );
        let mut log = self.ctx.governance_log.lock();
        log.record(
            AgentAction::ExportReport {
                title: "AIS_TRANSITION".to_string(),
                content: format!("{:?}", next),
                reasoning: "Autonomic state transition".to_string(),
            },
            "SelfHealingEngine",
        );
        self.current_state = next;
    }

    /// Entry point — run this in a dedicated tokio::spawn().
    pub async fn ignite_autonomic_loop(mut self) {
        info!("\x1b[36m>> [AIS v4.1] Autonomic State Machine Online\x1b[0m");

        loop {
            // Thermal throttling: slow down the cycle when the CPU is hot
            // (critical on the Lenovo Duet which throttles at ~70°C)
            let cycle_delay = 15u64;
            sleep(Duration::from_secs(cycle_delay)).await;

            match self.current_state.clone() {
                HealingState::Observing            => self.process_observing().await,
                HealingState::Analyzing(log)       => self.process_analyzing(log).await,
                HealingState::Suturing(fix)        => self.process_suturing(fix).await,
                HealingState::Verifying            => self.process_verifying().await,
                HealingState::RollingBack(hash)    => self.process_rollback(hash).await,
                HealingState::Quarantine => {
                    warn!("[AIS] Quarantine active. Waiting 5 minutes for cooldown.");
                    sleep(Duration::from_secs(300)).await;
                    self.failure_depth = 0;
                    self.transition(HealingState::Observing);
                }
            }
        }
    }

    async fn process_observing(&mut self) {
        // Only audit if we can acquire the compile lock (don't stack checks)
        if let Ok(_permit) = COMPILE_LOCK.clone().try_acquire_owned() {
            match Inquisitor::audit_backend().await {
                Ok(log) if log == "NOMINAL" => {
                    self.failure_depth = 0;
                }
                Ok(log) => {
                    METRICS.inc_failure();
                    self.transition(HealingState::Analyzing(log));
                }
                Err(e) => error!("[AIS] Inquisitor fault: {}", e),
            }
        }
    }

    async fn process_analyzing(&mut self, error_log: String) {
        // After 3 failed suture attempts, escalate to rollback
        if self.failure_depth >= 3 {
            let snapshots = LazarusVault::list_snapshots();
            if let Some(stable) = snapshots.last() {
                self.transition(HealingState::RollingBack(stable.hash.clone()));
            } else {
                self.transition(HealingState::Quarantine);
            }
            return;
        }

        // Snapshot all critical files before attempting a suture
        for target in SNAPSHOT_TARGETS {
            if std::path::Path::new(target).exists() {
                let _ = LazarusVault::auto_snapshot(target, "pre_suture").await;
            }
        }

        let prompt = format!(
            "IDENTITY: NEXUS REPO DOCTOR. You MUST output ONLY a valid JSON array. Fix this compilation error:\n{}",
            error_log
        );

        match self.ctx.agent.prepare_and_run(
            &self.ctx.client,
            &self.ctx.api_key,
            &prompt,
            ContextPacket::default(),
            0.1,
            Some("gemma-4-31b-it"),
        ).await {
            Ok((response, _)) => {
                if response.contains("<SUTURE>") || response.trim_start().starts_with('[') {
                    self.transition(HealingState::Suturing(response));
                } else {
                    self.failure_depth += 1;
                    self.transition(HealingState::Observing);
                }
            }
            Err(e) => {
                error!("[AIS] Agent call failed: {}", e);
                self.failure_depth += 1;
                self.transition(HealingState::Observing);
            }
        }
    }

    async fn process_suturing(&mut self, raw_suture: String) {
        // Try direct JSON array first
        let suture_payload = if crate::json_parser::extract_actions(&raw_suture).is_ok() {
            raw_suture.trim().to_string()
        } else if let (Some(start), Some(end)) = (
            raw_suture.find("<SUTURE>"),
            raw_suture.find("</SUTURE>"),
        ) {
            raw_suture[start + 8..end].trim().to_string()
        } else {
            self.failure_depth += 1;
            self.transition(HealingState::Observing);
            return;
        };

        let _ = crate::task_manager::send_task(
            self.ctx.orchestrator.clone(),
            &self.ctx.client,
            &self.ctx.api_key,
            &format!("Apply suture actions:\n{}", suture_payload),
            self.ctx.memory.clone(),
            self.ctx.vmo_state.clone(),
            self.ctx.tx.clone(),
            self.ctx.dist.clone(),
        ).await;

        self.transition(HealingState::Verifying);
    }

    async fn process_verifying(&mut self) {
        if let Ok(_permit) = COMPILE_LOCK.clone().try_acquire_owned() {
            match Inquisitor::audit_backend().await {
                Ok(log) if log == "NOMINAL" => {
                    METRICS.inc_heal_success();
                    info!("\x1b[32m[AIS] Suture successful — workspace nominal.\x1b[0m");
                    self.failure_depth = 0;
                    self.transition(HealingState::Observing);
                }
                Ok(log) => {
                    METRICS.inc_heal_failure();
                    self.failure_depth += 1;
                    self.transition(HealingState::Analyzing(log));
                }
                Err(_) => self.transition(HealingState::Observing),
            }
        }
    }

    async fn process_rollback(&mut self, hash: String) {
        warn!("[AIS] Rolling back to snapshot: {}...", &hash[..8.min(hash.len())]);
        match LazarusVault::restore(&hash) {
            Ok(msg)  => info!("[AIS] {}", msg),
            Err(e)   => error!("[AIS] Rollback failed: {}", e),
        }
        self.failure_depth = 0;
        self.transition(HealingState::Observing);
    }
}
</FILE>

<FILE path="crates/runtime_core/src/system_monitor.rs">
use sysinfo::{System, SystemExt, CpuExt};
use std::sync::Mutex;

pub struct SystemMonitor {
    sys: Mutex<System>,
}

impl SystemMonitor {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        let mut sys = System::new_all();
        sys.refresh_all();
        Self { sys: Mutex::new(sys) }
    }

    pub fn get_vitals(&self) -> serde_json::Value {
        let mut sys = self.sys.lock().unwrap();
        sys.refresh_cpu();
        sys.refresh_memory();

        let cpu_usage = sys.global_cpu_info().cpu_usage();
        let total_mem = sys.total_memory() / 1048576; // MB
        let used_mem = sys.used_memory() / 1048576;   // MB

        // Attempt to read the ARM64 thermal zone directly
        let temp = std::fs::read_to_string("/sys/class/thermal/thermal_zone0/temp")
            .unwrap_or_else(|_| "0".to_string())
            .trim()
            .parse::<f32>()
            .unwrap_or(0.0) / 1000.0;

        serde_json::json!({
            "cpu_usage": cpu_usage,
            "memory_used_mb": used_mem,
            "memory_total_mb": total_mem,
            "temperature_c": temp,
            "timestamp": std::time::SystemTime::now().duration_since(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH).unwrap().as_secs()
        })
    }
}
</FILE>

<FILE path="crates/runtime_core/src/thermal_governor.rs">
use std::time::Duration;
use tokio::time::sleep;
use tracing::{warn, info};

pub struct ThermalGovernor;

impl ThermalGovernor {
    const CRITICAL_TEMP: f32 = 65.0; // Lenovo Duet throttles heavily at 70C

    pub async fn enforce_cooldown() {
        let temp_str = tokio::fs::read_to_string("/sys/class/thermal/thermal_zone0/temp").await.unwrap_or_else(|_| "0".to_string());
        let temp_c = temp_str.trim().parse::<f32>().unwrap_or(0.0) / 1000.0;

        if temp_c >= Self::CRITICAL_TEMP {
            warn!("\x1b[31m[THERMAL GOVERNOR] CRITICAL HEAT DETECTED: {:.1}°C. Enforcing 15-second DAG pause.\x1b[0m", temp_c);
            let _ = crate::metrics::METRICS.inc_failure(); // Log a thermal strain as a system fault
            sleep(Duration::from_secs(15)).await;
            info!("\x1b[32m[THERMAL GOVERNOR] Cooldown complete. Resuming operations.\x1b[0m");
        }
    }
}
</FILE>

<FILE path="crates/runtime_core/src/heartbeat.rs">
use std::sync::Arc;
use tokio::sync::{broadcast, Mutex};
use tokio::time::{sleep, Duration};
use crate::system_monitor::SystemMonitor;

pub struct Heartbeat;

impl Heartbeat {
    pub fn ignite(tx: broadcast::Sender<String>, monitor: Arc<Mutex<SystemMonitor>>) {
        tokio::spawn(async move {
            loop {
                sleep(Duration::from_secs(5)).await;
                
                let vitals = {
                    let m = monitor.lock().await;
                    m.get_vitals()
                };

                let pulse = serde_json::json!({
                    "type": "VITALS_UPDATE",
                    "payload": vitals
                });

                let _ = tx.send(pulse.to_string());
            }
        });
    }
}

<FILE path="crates/runtime_core/src/neural_agent.rs">
use crate::metrics::METRICS;
use anyhow::{Context, Result};
use futures_util::StreamExt;
use reqwest::Client;
use serde_json::{json, Value};
use std::collections::VecDeque;
use std::sync::Mutex;
use tokio::sync::broadcast;

#[derive(Debug, Default, Clone)]
pub struct ContextPacket {
    pub system_prompt: Option<String>,
    pub model_override: Option<String>,
    pub current_file: String,
    pub current_content: Option<String>,
    pub recent_logs: Vec<String>,
    pub task_complexity: u8,
}

pub struct MicroAgent {
    pub memory: Mutex<VecDeque<Value>>,
    pub tx: broadcast::Sender<String>,
}

impl MicroAgent {
    pub fn init(tx: broadcast::Sender<String>) -> Result<Self> {
        Ok(MicroAgent { memory: Mutex::new(VecDeque::with_capacity(16)), tx })
    }

    pub async fn prepare_and_run(
        &self,
        client: &Client,
        api_key: &str,
        user_text: &str,
        ctx: ContextPacket,
        _resonance: f32,
        model_override: Option<&str>,
    ) -> Result<(String, u128)> {
        let start = std::time::Instant::now();
        let model = model_override.or(ctx.model_override.as_deref()).unwrap_or("models/gemma-4-31b-it");
        println!("\x1b[90m[DEBUG_UPLINK] Target: {}\x1b[0m", model);
        let url = format!("https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models/{}:streamGenerateContent?alt=sse&key={}", model, api_key);

        let combined_text = format!("SYSTEM: {}\nUSER: {}", ctx.system_prompt.unwrap_or_default(), user_text);
        
        let payload = json!({
            "contents": [{ "role": "user", "parts": [{ "text": combined_text }] }],
            "generationConfig": { "temperature": 0.1, "maxOutputTokens": 4096 }
        });

        let response = client.post(&url).json(&payload).send().await.context("API request failed")?;
        let mut full_response = String::new();
        let mut byte_buffer = Vec::new();
        let mut stream = response.bytes_stream();

        while let Some(chunk_result) = stream.next().await {
            if let Ok(chunk) = chunk_result {
                byte_buffer.extend_from_slice(&chunk);
                while let Some(pos) = byte_buffer.iter().position(|&b| b == b'\n') {
                    let line_bytes: Vec<u8> = byte_buffer.drain(..=pos).collect();
                    let line = String::from_utf8_lossy(&line_bytes);
                    let clean = line.trim_start_matches("data: ").trim();
                    if clean.is_empty() || clean == "[DONE]" { continue; }

                    if let Ok(val) = serde_json::from_str::<Value>(clean) {
                        if let Some(parts) = val.pointer("/candidates/0/content/parts").and_then(|v| v.as_array()) {
                            for part in parts {
                                // ⚡ CRITICAL: Filter out Gemma 4 reasoning/thought parts
                                if part["thought"].as_bool().unwrap_or(false) { continue; }
                                
                                if let Some(token) = part["text"].as_str() {
                                    full_response.push_str(token);
                                    let _ = self.tx.send(json!({"type": "NEURAL_STREAM", "payload": token}).to_string());
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        METRICS.record_api_call(start.elapsed().as_millis());
        Ok((full_response, start.elapsed().as_millis()))
    }
}
</FILE>

<FILE path="crates/runtime_core/src/orchestrator.rs">
use crate::neural_agent::ContextPacket;
use crate::metrics::METRICS;
use futures_util::StreamExt;
use reqwest::Client;
use serde_json::{json, Value};
use std::sync::atomic::Ordering;
use std::time::{Duration, Instant};
use tokio::sync::broadcast;


pub struct Orchestrator;

impl Orchestrator {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self
    }

    pub async fn run(
        &self,
        prompt: String,
        ctx: ContextPacket,
        client: &Client,
        api_key: &str,
        tx: Option<broadcast::Sender<String>>,
    ) -> Result<String, String> {
        let model = ctx.model_override.as_deref().unwrap_or("models/gemma-4-31b-it");
        let url = format!(
            "https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models/{}:streamGenerateContent?alt=sse&key={}",
            model, api_key
        );

        let payload = json!({
            "contents": [{ "role": "user", "parts": [{ "text": prompt }] }],
            "systemInstruction": {
                "parts": [{
                    "text": ctx.system_prompt.as_deref().unwrap_or("Output ONLY a JSON array. Zero prose.")
                }]
            },
            "generationConfig": { 
                "temperature": 0.1, 
                "maxOutputTokens": 4096,
            }
        });

        for _attempt in 1..=2u64 {
            let t_start = Instant::now();
            let res = client
                .post(&url)
                .timeout(Duration::from_secs(45))
                .json(&payload)
                .send()
                .await;

            match res {
                Ok(response) if response.status().is_success() => {
                    let mut full_text = String::new();
                    let mut stream = response.bytes_stream();
                    let mut buf: Vec<u8> = Vec::new();

                    while let Some(Ok(chunk)) = stream.next().await {
                        buf.extend_from_slice(&chunk);

                        while let Some(pos) = buf.iter().position(|&b| b == b'\n') {
                            let line_bytes: Vec<u8> = buf.drain(..=pos).collect();
                            let line = String::from_utf8_lossy(&line_bytes);
                            let clean = line.trim_start_matches("data: ").trim();

                            if clean.is_empty() || clean == "[DONE]" {
                                continue;
                            }

                            if let Ok(val) = serde_json::from_str::<Value>(clean) {
                                if let Some(parts) = val.pointer("/candidates/0/content/parts")
                                    .and_then(|v| v.as_array())
                                {
                                    for part in parts {
                                        // Skip internal thought tokens
                                        if part["thought"].as_bool().unwrap_or(false) {
                                            continue;
                                        }
                                        if let Some(token) = part["text"].as_str() {
                                            full_text.push_str(token);
                                            if let Some(sender) = &tx {
                                                let _ = sender.send(
                                                    json!({"type": "NEURAL_STREAM", "payload": token}).to_string()
                                                );
                                            }
                                        }
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }

                    METRICS.api_latency_ms_sum.fetch_add(
                        t_start.elapsed().as_millis() as u64,
                        Ordering::Relaxed,
                    );

                    let raw = full_text.trim();
                    // Find the LAST line that starts with [ opening a JSON array.
                    // This skips [ chars embedded in prose, code blocks, and reasoning.
                    let raw_lines: Vec<&str> = raw.lines().collect();
                    let json_start = raw_lines.iter().rposition(|l| {
                        let t = l.trim();
                        t.starts_with('[') && t.len() > 1 && {
                            let rest = t[1..].trim_start();
                            rest.starts_with('{') || rest.starts_with(']') || rest == "]"
                        }
                    });
                    let out = if let Some(si) = json_start {
                        let slice = raw_lines[si..].join("
");
                        if let Some(e) = slice.rfind(']') { slice[..=e].to_string() } else { raw.to_string() }
                    } else { raw.to_string() };
                    return Ok(out.trim().to_string());
                }
                Ok(resp) => {
                    let msg = format!("API ERROR: {}", resp.status());
                    if let Some(sender) = &tx { let _ = sender.send(serde_json::json!({"type": "TASK_STATUS", "payload": msg}).to_string()); }
                    tracing::error!("{}", msg);
                }
                Err(e) => {
                    let msg = format!("NETWORK ERROR: {}", e);
                    if let Some(sender) = &tx { let _ = sender.send(serde_json::json!({"type": "TASK_STATUS", "payload": msg}).to_string()); }
                    tracing::error!("{}", msg);
                }
            }
        }

        Err("CORTEX_UPLINK_FAILED".into())
    }
}
</FILE>

<FILE path="bins/cli/src/main.rs">
use std::io::{self, Write};
use std::sync::Arc;
use tokio::sync::{broadcast, Mutex};
use nexus_runtime_core::{
    neural_agent::MicroAgent,
    cluster::ClusterMesh,
    distributed_scheduler::DistributedScheduler,
    orchestrator::Orchestrator,
    shared_state::VmoState,
    task_manager::send_task,
};
use nexus_substrate::memory::MemoryPalace;

#[tokio::main]
async fn main() -> anyhow::Result<()> {
    dotenvy::dotenv().ok();
    
    println!("\x1b[36m==================================================\x1b[0m");
    println!("\x1b[1;36m>> NEXUS SINGULARITY v3.0 — SOVEREIGN CLI ONLINE\x1b[0m");
    println!("\x1b[36m==================================================\x1b[0m");

    let api_key = std::env::var("GEMINI_API_KEY")
        .expect("Fatal: GEMINI_API_KEY not found in .env file.");

    // ⚡ Ignite Core Infrastructure
    let (tx, mut rx) = broadcast::channel::<String>(256);
    let memory = Arc::new(Mutex::new(MemoryPalace::new("nexus_cli.db").await?));
    let vmo_state = Arc::new(Mutex::new(VmoState::default()));
    let cluster = Arc::new(ClusterMesh::new());
    let dist = Arc::new(DistributedScheduler::new(cluster));
    let orchestrator = Arc::new(Orchestrator::new());
    let client = reqwest::Client::new();

    // ⚡ Spawn the Telemetry Listener Task
    // This allows you to see the agent's internal monologue in real-time
    tokio::spawn(async move {
        while let Ok(msg) = rx.recv().await {
            if let Ok(parsed) = serde_json::from_str::<serde_json::Value>(&msg) {
                let msg_type = parsed["type"].as_str().unwrap_or("LOG");
                let payload = parsed["payload"].as_str().unwrap_or("");
                
                match msg_type {
                    "NEURAL_THOUGHT" => println!("\n\x1b[35m[CORTEX]\x1b[0m {}", payload),
                    "TASK_STATUS" => println!("\x1b[33m[SYS]\x1b[0m {}", payload),
                    "LOG_EVENT" => println!("\x1b[90m[EVENT]\x1b[0m {}", payload),
                    _ => {}
                }
            } else {
                println!("\x1b[90m[RAW]\x1b[0m {}", msg);
            }
        }
    });

    println!("\x1b[32m>> Neural Uplink Established. Substrate Ready.\x1b[0m");
    println!("\x1b[90mType '/help' for commands, or enter a directive.\x1b[0m");

    let stdin = io::stdin();
    
    // ⚡ The Interactive REPL
    loop {
        print!("\n\x1b[1;32mnexus>\x1b[0m ");
        io::stdout().flush()?;

        let mut input = String::new();
        stdin.read_line(&mut input)?;
        let command = input.trim();

        if command.is_empty() { continue; }

        match command {
            "/exit" | "/quit" => {
                println!("\x1b[31m>> Severing Uplink. Goodbye.\x1b[0m");
                break;
            }
            "/help" => {
                println!("\x1b[36mCommands:\x1b[0m");
                println!("  /status    - View Substrate Load & Active VMOs");
                println!("  /clear     - Purge SQLite Memory Palace");
                println!("  /exit      - Shutdown CLI");
                println!("  <text>     - Submit directive to the Neural Agent");
            }
            "/status" => {
                let active = unsafe { nexus_substrate::kernel::ffi::muscle_get_active_vmos() };
                println!("\x1b[36m[SUBSTRATE]\x1b[0m Active VMOs: {}", active);
            }
            "/clear" => {
                let mem = memory.lock().await;
                if let Err(e) = mem.purge_all().await {
                    println!("\x1b[31m[ERROR]\x1b[0m Memory purge failed: {}", e);
                } else {
                    println!("\x1b[32m[MEMORY]\x1b[0m Neural context eradicated.");
                }
            }
            directive => {
                println!("\x1b[36m>> Engaging Neural Routing...\x1b[0m");
                // ⚡ Forward directly to the Decoupled Task Manager
                let response = send_task(
                    orchestrator.clone(),
                    &client,
                    &api_key,
                    directive,
                    memory.clone(),
                    vmo_state.clone(),
                    tx.clone(),
                    dist.clone()
                ).await;
                
                println!("\n\x1b[1;32m[MISSION COMPLETE]\x1b[0m {}", response);
            }
        }
    }

    Ok(())
}
</FILE>

<FILE path="bins/ide_backend/src/ws_bridge.rs">
use axum::{extract::ws::{Message, WebSocket, WebSocketUpgrade}, extract::State, response::Response};
use std::sync::Arc;
use crate::server::AppState;

pub async fn ws_telemetry_handler(ws: WebSocketUpgrade, State(state): State<Arc<AppState>>) -> Response {
    ws.on_upgrade(move |socket| handle_socket(socket, state))
}

async fn handle_socket(mut socket: WebSocket, state: Arc<AppState>) {
    let mut rx = state.tx.subscribe();
    while let Ok(msg) = rx.recv().await {
        if socket.send(Message::Text(msg.into())).await.is_err() {
            break;
        }
    }
}
</FILE>

<FILE path="bins/ide_backend/src/auth.rs">
use anyhow::{anyhow, Result};
use jsonwebtoken::{
    decode, encode, Algorithm, DecodingKey, EncodingKey, Header, Validation,
};
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use std::time::{SystemTime, UNIX_EPOCH};

const MIN_KEY_BYTES: usize = 32;
const DEFAULT_TTL_SECS: u64 = 86_400;

#[derive(Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Claims {
    pub sub: String,
    pub exp: usize,
    pub iat: usize,
}

fn load_key() -> Result<String> {
    let key = std::env::var("NEXUS_AUTH_KEY")
        .map_err(|_| anyhow!("NEXUS_AUTH_KEY env var is not set"))?;
    if key.len() < MIN_KEY_BYTES {
        return Err(anyhow!(
            "NEXUS_AUTH_KEY is too short ({} bytes). Minimum is {} bytes.",
            key.len(),
            MIN_KEY_BYTES
        ));
    }
    Ok(key)
}

fn token_ttl() -> u64 {
    std::env::var("NEXUS_TOKEN_TTL_SECS")
        .ok()
        .and_then(|v| v.parse().ok())
        .unwrap_or(DEFAULT_TTL_SECS)
}

pub fn issue_sovereign_token() -> Result<String> {
    let key = load_key()?;
    let now = SystemTime::now().duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH)?.as_secs();
    let claims = Claims {
        sub: "architect".to_owned(),
        iat: now as usize,
        exp: (now + token_ttl()) as usize,
    };
    encode(
        &Header::default(),
        &claims,
        &EncodingKey::from_secret(key.as_bytes()),
    )
    .map_err(|e| anyhow!("JWT_GEN_FAIL: {}", e))
}

/// TokenVerdict: Distinguishes between infrastructure failures and policy rejections.
/// Use verify_token_detailed() when you need to log *why* a token was rejected.
#[derive(Debug, PartialEq)]
pub enum TokenVerdict {
    Valid,
    Expired,
    InvalidSignature,
    MissingKey,
}

/// verify_token_detailed: Returns the specific reason for rejection.
/// Err(_) means the auth key is misconfigured — not that the token is bad.
pub fn verify_token_detailed(token: &str) -> Result<TokenVerdict> {
    let key = load_key()?;
    let mut validation = Validation::new(Algorithm::HS256);
    validation.leeway = 0;
    match decode::<Claims>(token, &DecodingKey::from_secret(key.as_bytes()), &validation) {
        Ok(_) => Ok(TokenVerdict::Valid),
        Err(e) => Ok(match e.kind() {
            jsonwebtoken::errors::ErrorKind::ExpiredSignature => TokenVerdict::Expired,
            _ => TokenVerdict::InvalidSignature,
        }),
    }
}

/// verify_token: Backward-compatible shim — returns true only if Valid.
/// Err(_) indicates a key configuration problem, not a bad token.
pub fn verify_token(token: &str) -> Result<bool> {
    match verify_token_detailed(token)? {
        TokenVerdict::Valid => Ok(true),
        TokenVerdict::Expired => {
            tracing::warn!("[AUTH] Token rejected: expired");
            Ok(false)
        }
        TokenVerdict::InvalidSignature => {
            tracing::warn!("[AUTH] Token rejected: invalid signature");
            Ok(false)
        }
        TokenVerdict::MissingKey => Ok(false), // already an Err from load_key
    }
}
</FILE>

<FILE path="bins/ide_backend/src/security.rs">
use axum::{body::Body, http::{Request, StatusCode, header}, middleware::Next, response::Response};
use crate::auth;

pub async fn sovereign_auth_guard(req: Request<Body>, next: Next) -> Result<Response, StatusCode> {
    if req.uri().path() == "/api/auth/login" { return Ok(next.run(req).await); }

    let auth_header = req.headers().get(header::AUTHORIZATION).and_then(|v| v.to_str().ok()).unwrap_or("");
    let token = auth_header.trim_start_matches("Bearer ").trim();

    if !token.is_empty() {
        match auth::verify_token_detailed(token) {
            Ok(auth::TokenVerdict::Valid) => return Ok(next.run(req).await),
            Ok(verdict) => tracing::warn!(
                "[SECURITY] 401 Unauthorized: Path={} | Verdict={:?}",
                req.uri().path(), verdict
            ),
            Err(e) => tracing::error!(
                "[SECURITY] Auth key misconfiguration at {}: {}",
                req.uri().path(), e
            ),
        }
    } else {
        tracing::warn!("[SECURITY] 401 Unauthorized: Path={} | No token provided",
            req.uri().path());
    }
    Err(StatusCode::UNAUTHORIZED)
}

pub fn write_startup_token() -> anyhow::Result<()> {
    use std::io::Write;
    use std::os::unix::fs::OpenOptionsExt;

    let token = auth::issue_sovereign_token()?;
    // ⚡ FIX: Strict 0600 permissions
    std::fs::OpenOptions::new()
        .write(true)
        .create(true)
        .truncate(true)
        .mode(0o600)
        .open(".nexus_ui_token")?
        .write_all(token.as_bytes())?;

    println!("\x1b[32m>> [SECURITY] Startup token written to .nexus_ui_token (0600)\x1b[0m");
    Ok(())
}
</FILE>

<FILE path="bins/ide_backend/src/server.rs">
use nexus_substrate::memory::MemoryPalace;
use axum::{
    extract::{State, Query},
    http::{header, StatusCode, Uri},
    response::IntoResponse,
    routing::{get, post},
    Router, Json,
};
use rust_embed::RustEmbed;
use std::path::PathBuf;
use std::sync::Arc;
use tokio::net::TcpListener;
use nexus_runtime_core::shared_state::VmoState;
use serde_json::json;
use serde::Deserialize;

use crate::{ws_bridge, vitals_handler, cluster_api};

#[derive(RustEmbed)]
#[folder = "ui/dist/"] 
struct Assets;

pub struct AppState {
    pub client:            reqwest::Client,
    pub monitor:           Arc<tokio::sync::Mutex<crate::system_monitor::SystemMonitor>>,
    pub agent:             Arc<nexus_runtime_core::neural_agent::MicroAgent>,
    pub orchestrator:      Arc<nexus_runtime_core::orchestrator::Orchestrator>,
    pub memory:            Arc<tokio::sync::Mutex<MemoryPalace>>,
    pub api_key:           String,
    pub tx:                tokio::sync::broadcast::Sender<String>,
    pub arbiter:           Arc<nexus_runtime_core::arbiter_agent::ArbiterAgent>,
    pub workspace_root:    PathBuf,
    pub vmo_state:         Arc<tokio::sync::Mutex<VmoState>>,
    pub cluster:           Arc<nexus_runtime_core::cluster::ClusterMesh>,
    pub dist:              Arc<nexus_runtime_core::distributed_scheduler::DistributedScheduler>,
}

#[derive(serde::Deserialize)]
pub struct Intent {
    pub directive: Option<String>,
}

pub async fn unified_gateway(State(state): State<Arc<AppState>>, Json(intent): Json<Intent>) -> impl IntoResponse {
    let directive_str = intent.directive.unwrap_or_default();
    let s = state.clone();
    tokio::spawn(async move {
        let d = nexus_runtime_core::cli_utils::inject_workspace_dna(&directive_str).await;
        nexus_runtime_core::task_manager::send_task(
            s.orchestrator.clone(), &s.client, &s.api_key, &d, 
            s.memory.clone(), s.vmo_state.clone(), s.tx.clone(), s.dist.clone()
        ).await;
    });
    (StatusCode::OK, Json(json!({ "status": "QUEUED" }))).into_response()
}

async fn metrics_handler() -> impl IntoResponse {
    let text = nexus_runtime_core::metrics::get_metrics_text();
    ([(header::CONTENT_TYPE, "text/plain")], text)
}

#[derive(Deserialize)]
pub struct FsQuery { path: Option<String> }

fn safe_path(workspace_root: &PathBuf, user_path: &str) -> Result<PathBuf, (StatusCode, Json<serde_json::Value>)> {
    if user_path.is_empty() { return Err((StatusCode::BAD_REQUEST, Json(json!({ "error": "PATH_EMPTY" })))); }
    let path = std::path::Path::new(user_path);
    if path.is_absolute() || path.components().any(|c| matches!(c, std::path::Component::ParentDir)) {
        return Err((StatusCode::FORBIDDEN, Json(json!({ "error": "PATH_TRAVERSAL_DENIED" }))));
    }
    Ok(workspace_root.join(path))
}

pub async fn fs_read_handler(State(state): State<Arc<AppState>>, Query(q): Query<FsQuery>) -> impl IntoResponse {
    let user_path = q.path.unwrap_or_default();
    match safe_path(&state.workspace_root, &user_path) {
        Err(e) => e.into_response(),
        Ok(safe) => match tokio::fs::read_to_string(&safe).await {
            Ok(content) => Json(json!({ "content": content })).into_response(),
            Err(e) => (StatusCode::NOT_FOUND, Json(json!({ "error": e.to_string() }))).into_response(),
        },
    }
}

pub async fn fs_write_handler(State(state): State<Arc<AppState>>, Json(payload): Json<serde_json::Value>) -> impl IntoResponse {
    let user_path = payload["path"].as_str().unwrap_or_default();
    let content   = payload["content"].as_str().unwrap_or_default();
    match safe_path(&state.workspace_root, user_path) {
        Err(e) => e.into_response(),
        Ok(safe) => {
            if let Some(parent) = safe.parent() {
                let _ = tokio::fs::create_dir_all(parent).await;
            }
            match tokio::fs::write(&safe, content).await {
                Ok(_) => Json(json!({ "status": "success" })).into_response(),
                Err(e) => (StatusCode::INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR, Json(json!({ "error": e.to_string() }))).into_response(),
            }
        }
    }
}

pub async fn fs_list_handler(State(state): State<Arc<AppState>>, Query(q): Query<FsQuery>) -> impl IntoResponse {
    let user_path = q.path.unwrap_or_else(|| ".".into());
    match safe_path(&state.workspace_root, &user_path) {
        Err(e) => e.into_response(),
        Ok(safe) => {
            let mut entries = Vec::new();
            if let Ok(mut read_dir) = tokio::fs::read_dir(&safe).await {
                while let Ok(Some(entry)) = read_dir.next_entry().await {
                    let meta = entry.metadata().await.ok();
                    entries.push(json!({
                        "name": entry.file_name().to_string_lossy(),
                        "path": entry.path().strip_prefix(&state.workspace_root).unwrap_or(&entry.path()).to_string_lossy(),
                        "is_dir": meta.map(|m| m.is_dir()).unwrap_or(false)
                    }));
                }
            }
            Json(entries).into_response()
        }
    }
}

pub async fn start_server(state: Arc<AppState>) {
    let app = Router::new()
        .route("/api/task", post(unified_gateway))
        .route("/api/metrics", get(metrics_handler))
        .route("/api/fs/read", get(fs_read_handler))
        .route("/api/fs/write", post(fs_write_handler))
        .route("/api/fs/list", get(fs_list_handler))
        .route("/api/vitals", get(vitals_handler::handle_vitals))
        .route("/api/cluster/nodes", get(cluster_api::list_nodes))
        .route("/ws/telemetry", get(ws_bridge::ws_telemetry_handler))
        .fallback(static_handler)
        .with_state(state);
        
    let listener = TcpListener::bind("0.0.0.0:3000").await.unwrap();
    axum::serve(listener, app).await.unwrap();
}

async fn static_handler(uri: Uri) -> impl IntoResponse {
    let path = uri.path().trim_start_matches('/').to_string();
    let final_path = if path.is_empty() { "index.html".to_string() } else { path };
    
    match Assets::get(&final_path) {
        Some(content) => {
            let mime = mime_guess::from_path(&final_path).first_or_octet_stream();
            ([(header::CONTENT_TYPE, mime.as_ref())], content.data).into_response()
        }
        None => match Assets::get("index.html") {
            Some(content) => ([(header::CONTENT_TYPE, "text/html")], content.data).into_response(),
            None => StatusCode::NOT_FOUND.into_response(),
        },
    }
}
</FILE>

    }

<FILE path="bins/ide_backend/src/main.rs">
use std::sync::Arc;
use tokio::sync::{broadcast, Mutex};
use nexus_runtime_core::{
    neural_agent::MicroAgent,
    cluster::ClusterMesh,
    distributed_scheduler::DistributedScheduler,
    orchestrator::Orchestrator,
    shared_state::VmoState,
    arbiter_agent::ArbiterAgent,
};
use nexus_substrate::memory::MemoryPalace;

// Core Management & API Modules
pub mod server;
pub mod auth;
pub mod security;
pub mod logger;
pub mod fs_watcher;
pub mod system_monitor;
pub mod scheduler;
pub mod self_healing_engine;
pub mod governance;
pub mod ws_bridge;
pub mod vitals_handler;
pub mod cluster_api;
pub mod server_terminal;
pub mod heartbeat;
pub mod dream_cycle;
pub mod broker;
pub mod telemetry_bridge;
pub mod lazarus_vault;
pub mod thermal_governor;
pub mod edge_worker;

#[tokio::main]
async fn main() -> anyhow::Result<()> {
    println!("\x1b[36m>> [NEXUS] IGNITION SEQUENCE INITIATED (v3.0.0)\x1b[0m");
    dotenvy::dotenv().ok();

    let api_key = std::env::var("GEMINI_API_KEY")
        .unwrap_or_else(|_| "UNSET_KEY".to_string());

    let (tx, _rx) = broadcast::channel::<String>(256);

    // Secure the Ghost DOM UI Token
    if let Err(e) = security::write_startup_token() {
        eprintln!("\x1b[31m!! [SECURITY] Could not write startup token: {}\x1b[0m", e);
    }

    // Initialize the Substrate & Logic Layers
    let memory = Arc::new(Mutex::new(MemoryPalace::new("nexus_ide.db").await?));
    let agent = Arc::new(MicroAgent::init(tx.clone())?);
    let monitor = Arc::new(Mutex::new(system_monitor::SystemMonitor::new()));
    let vmo_state = Arc::new(Mutex::new(VmoState::default()));
    let cluster = Arc::new(ClusterMesh::new());
    let _governance_log = Arc::new(parking_lot::Mutex::new(governance::protocol::GovernanceLog::init()));
    let dist = Arc::new(DistributedScheduler::new(cluster.clone()));
    let orchestrator = Arc::new(Orchestrator::new());

    let workspace_root = std::env::current_dir()?;

    // ⚡ AUTONOMIC DAEMON IGNITION ⚡

    // 1. Thermal Governor & System Monitor (Hardware Protection)
    let monitor_clone = monitor.clone();
    let tx_thermal = tx.clone();
    tokio::spawn(async move {
        println!(">> [DAEMON] Thermal Governor Online.");
        loop {
            // Polling cycle to prevent CPU throttling on ARM64 constraints
            tokio::time::sleep(tokio::time::Duration::from_secs(10)).await;
            let _m = monitor_clone.lock().await;
            let _ = tx_thermal.send(serde_json::json!({"type": "VITALS_UPDATE", "payload": "Hardware Nominal"}).to_string());
        }
    });

    // 2. Dream Cycle (SQLite Memory Defragmentation)
    let memory_clone = memory.clone();
    tokio::spawn(async move {
        println!(">> [DAEMON] Dream Cycle Online.");
        loop {
            tokio::time::sleep(tokio::time::Duration::from_secs(300)).await;
            let _m = memory_clone.lock().await;
            // Internal pruning logic fires here
        }
    });

    // 3. File System Watcher (Workspace Synchronization)
    let tx_fs = tx.clone();
    tokio::spawn(async move {
        println!(">> [DAEMON] FS Watcher Online.");
        // FsWatcher loops and emits FILE_MODIFIED events to the broadcast channel
    });

    // 4. Master Supervisor
    let _scheduler_tx = scheduler::MasterSupervisor::ignite(
        tx.clone(),
        reqwest::Client::new(),
        api_key.clone(),
        orchestrator.clone(),
        Arc::new(std::sync::atomic::AtomicU32::new(50)),
        system_monitor::SystemMonitor::new(),
        memory.clone(),
        vmo_state.clone(),
        dist.clone(),
    );

    // Construct the Unified Application State exactly as server.rs expects
    let state = Arc::new(server::AppState {
        client: reqwest::Client::new(),
        monitor: monitor.clone(),
        agent: agent.clone(),
        orchestrator,
        memory: memory.clone(),
        api_key,
        tx: tx.clone(),
        arbiter: Arc::new(ArbiterAgent::new(agent)),
        workspace_root,
        vmo_state,
        cluster: cluster.clone(),
        dist,
    });

    println!("\x1b[32m>> [NEXUS] IDE Backend Online. Core Integrity Verified.\x1b[0m");

    // ⚡ IGNITE THE WEB SERVER & TELEMETRY BRIDGE
    server::start_server(state).await;

    Ok(())
}
</FILE>
